+# T2 E02 · Let the 8B author inspect its sources
+
+Revision 3, correcting the remaining F1 and F4 details. Design for wo-ultimaagent-20261101-0021, 2026-11-02. One candidate for independent design review. Source10, model calls, actual execution and T3 release = false. The original fixed question, required factual coverage, negative cases, real file, real network and Owner gates stand.
+
+## 1. What changes at the product boundary
+
+After successful SDK search and GET, give the current author a compact catalogue. Let it choose windows of the already fetched sources within one NativeReAct conversation. The next generation receives each real inspector observation alongside the original question. Once sufficient distinct sources have actually been shown, the author can submit one report. This makes “never inspected”, “inspected but uncited” and “cited but unsupported” distinguishable. No server-authored facts, model score or predetermined source order enters the task.
+
+**PROVED.** Source9 used 13,888 prompt tokens and selected S1 in all five sections. None of 22 claims had adequate support at its cited location. Duplicate-source rejection prevented the file. The closed S3 and S4 single-source stages both ended normally with unused and zero facts. S4 already had a direct execution card and local-role instructions. On the same product bytes E01 and E03 passed three fixed cases apiece through ordinary SDK tool feedback. These results establish tool execution in those cases. Factual improvement from navigation is a **HYPOTHESIS**. Whether this 8B will look at relevant windows, distinguish the Jarvis.ceo donor from the OpenJarvis floor, explain conditions and alternatives, and finish on time is **UNKNOWN**. The closed offline attempt entered zero comparisons. Native100ns versus legacy CIM precision mismatch is established, outer stall cause unknown, terminal missing and original base identity uncaptured.
+
+| Location and proposed interface | Responsibility |
+|---|---|
+| work_service.py, existing submit_work(...) | Preserve /ask and work JSON and trusted startup gates. Only explicit research uses v3. Start the same 260 s deadline at entry. Collect once with existing collect_research including search/GET policies, attempts, duplicate and failure accounting. Freeze the exact captured request material and collection before composition. Disable collection capabilities for composition. |
+| work_research.py, prepare_navigation(collection, binding, scanners) → frozen CatalogueV3 | Trusted preparation verifies successful GET call linkage, request/session/task, final URL, normalized URL, body and decoded SHA-256. Preserve collected bytes and projection separately in the existing restricted evidence boundary. Build a second, safe immutable view as below. Immutable canonical compact JSON in UTF-8 (sorted keys, no NaN), SHA-256 over exact bytes. |
+| work_tools.py, WorkInspectTool(BaseTool).execute(**params) → ToolResult, WorkResearchWriteTool, WorkLedger | Register only a tool class under ultima_work_research_inspect in the existing ToolRegistry. Within bind_work the SDK name builder constructs the inspector and writer; their zero-argument constructors enroll those exact instances during the single permitted build epoch. Only the enrolled writer constructs the nested WorkWriteTool on that caller thread. Inspector captures only safe catalogue and restricted ledger operations, never browser/provider/path/raw catalogue. Writer holds frozen original evidence for validation. Worker execute uses captured instances, never resolves authority from worker ContextVar or model input. Keep mutable generation, receipts and dispatches under the per-request ledger RLock. |
+| agent_server.py, existing _run_work_agent and _ResearchEngineView.generate(messages, **kwargs) | Preserve one Jarvis.ask_full(query, agent='native_react', tools=[inspector,writer], context=False, max_tokens=allowance). Validate actual installed wrapper shapes and two bound tools. Research view retains the real system/tool instructions, user query and SDK history, replacing the one pinned output-format prefix and adding compact phase rules as specified below. Refuse unexpected templates, extra exemplars, rewritten or compressed history, roles or capability descriptions. No synthetic observations. Add a request-local monotonically increasing generation ID and ledger operations expect(seq,name,args_hash), claim(seq,name,args_hash,adapter_id), confirm_end(seq,event), promote_visible(generation,wire_hash), freeze_for_write(generation) and seal(reason). Keep SDK START/END correlation on caller thread under _ask_lock. |
+| work_research.py, resolve_v3(submitted, issued_snapshot, collection), verify_research_artifacts(..., required_version=3) | Resolve only the frozen current-request issued witnesses. Render model text with the validated evidence and server URLs. Link SDK write, exact original path and reopened bytes. For the fixed case bind the independently specified basename research.md and fresh request root before generation; reject another writer path and verify that exact file outside the process. Keep work_verifier.verify_artifacts as the file check. |
+
+The installed sdk.py:271,443 builds fresh tool objects then runs the registered NativeReActAgent. native_react.py:225–360 calls generate(messages), parses the action, calls ToolExecutor.execute(ToolCall), appends “Observation: ” plus ToolResult.content as a USER message and continues. tools/_stubs.py:381–462 publishes START/END on the caller and runs BaseTool.execute on the bounded worker pool. ToolSpec, ToolResult and registry creation stay on that SDK path. Agent name alone does not identify a request.
+
+**SDK constraint.** Jarvis.__init__ accepts config/engine_key/model and ask_full accepts tool names. Neither accepts a per-call engine instance. Retain the existing checked request-only shallow copies in _borrow_research and the owned leaf/client, as an explicit installed-version dependency. No modification of the singleton, SDK source, tool executor, shared bus policy, scanner settings, engine defaults or /ask chat path. Private wrapper mismatch stops before composition. The agent's public constructor accepts an engine but changing to a separately assembled agent would require proving all existing security and telemetry wiring again. Pin the six SDK source hashes in preparation/sdk-sources.json and the additional used registry/tools/guard/scanner files for future candidate review.
+
+## 2. Exactly what can be inspected and cited
+
+| Boundary | v3 rule |
+|---|---|
+| Trusted input | Up to five already accepted GETs. For a source truncated at GET, >256 KiB decoded UTF-8, >1,024 lines, invalid hash, or unsupported coordinate map, mark unavailable with reason. Identity/hash or GET-ledger mismatch aborts request. Accept raw-coordinate code only for identity text/body with LF/CRLF. Accept documented HTML in extracted-text coordinates only for the original documented suffixes under the reconstruction rule below. Never map HTML projected line numbers to raw markup. Unknown suffix can be listed but cannot supply a witness. Require three mutually distinct normalized URLs and decoded-body digests with at least one legal final-page card each before the first POST. |
+| Security before any tool output | Inside trusted preparation use the same configured SDK scanners on the complete original text and on individual numbered lines. In BLOCK mode a full-text finding blocks the source. Otherwise remove entire affected lines even under WARN. For each coordinate domain require sequential whole-text redact output to equal the concatenated individually redacted lines with original separators, and scan the redacted projection for residual findings. If mapping, scanner behavior or separators disagree, deny the whole source. Exclude dirty lines from all text, operation labels and anchors, keep only line number/reason. Scan catalogue labels, action descriptions and complete serialized observation before exposure. Security exclusions cannot be reversed by another page. Frozen original data remain available only to trusted verification. |
+| Catalogue ≤4,096 UTF-8 bytes | Per source give S label, safe final host/path identity, suffix, line count, available/omitted reason and neutral navigation landmarks. Landmarks are up to eight evenly spaced eligible anchor coordinates and literal operation names in source order (for docs, coordinates only). Drop whole landmarks from the end round-robin until fit. If mandatory identities cannot fit or safely identify provenance, fail. No claim, ranking, preferred sources or citation handles. Catalogue is data. |
+| ToolSpec | Closed object {source_id: S1…S5, start_line: integer 1…1024}. One call reads at most 24 consecutive original line positions starting there, never beyond EOF. Return compact JSON {v,source_id,view_id,start,end,total_lines,lines,cards,omissions,next_start}. lines are [original_line,text], cards are [ref,kind,operation,anchor_first,anchor_last,citation_first,citation_last]. Missing lines explicitly tagged security or size; next_start advances past the examined range. No URL, file path, raw read, arbitrary search string, network, queue or memory facility. |
+| Size and selection | At most 3,072 serialized UTF-8 bytes including metadata per observation, eight cards, six successful or attempted reads per request, 18,432 bytes total. First reserve bounded omission-run metadata, then add full lines in order, skipping oversized individual lines; never split a line. Add cards only from actually fitting lines, stopping at card or byte cap. Remove whole trailing entries as required to fit, record size omissions or count of omitted cards, fail if metadata alone exceeds cap. Duplicate same start/source or invalid requests fail, no replay. Different starts may overlap, all calls count. |
+| Evidence construction | In trusted preparation reuse _anchors, _python_witnesses, _js_tokens, _fact_evidence and complete-statement limits from source9. Validate anchors on original source, filter through the safe lines. Per window choose in ascending (first,last,kind,symbol) order, no relevance selection. Use up to eight contiguous actually returned lines around the ≤8-line anchor as in v2, stopping at every gap. A model may discuss local conditions/alternatives visible in that citation and submit separate atomic claims with other issued witnesses. Nearby activity alone does not prove a control dependency. |
+
+Keep the known JS/TS this/super receiver defect outside this candidate. This isolates the new ability to inspect and keeps existing anchor semantics. Missing operations cannot be cited as direct witnesses. An explicitly scoped future receiver change would need baseline v2 and stage projections, real multiline this/super and ordinary receiver positives, broken continuations, strings, comments and unrelated-keyword negatives, exact-token/anchor and final-visible diffs, unchanged Python support and old receipt reopening. No offline gain is claimed. Navigation need not yield enough expressible evidence in a given case.
+
+## 3. Issuance, one write and reopening
+
+| State under request ledger lock | Admission and terminal behavior |
+|---|---|
+| READY → EXPECT(tool,args_digest,seq) | Each generation validates its exact prior assistant/observation chain against the ledger. Closed ReAct JSON union contains one action with object arguments or one nonempty final_answer. Start with inspect/refuse. Count only distinct URL/body identities with at least one actually visible, issued card; the pending third read is provisional only for constructing this turn’s sampler. At the read limit permit only eligible write or final. Prewrite final means refusal/failure. No arbitrary final-answer success. |
+| EXPECT → START → STAGED | Caller-thread subscription checks bound object identity, SDK tool name, canonical args digest and single expected sequence; allocate dispatch UUID at START. Insist on one pending dispatch. Worker atomically claims matching (request,seq,tool,args_digest), records adapter UUID, hashes, complete safe observation and prospective handles before returning. END must succeed once, match worker record by linked UUID, exact observation bytes, caller and request. Never infer success from reply text or ask_full's stripped tool_results. Timeout, absent START/END, out-of-order or late receipt seals first failure, bars further admission. |
+| STAGED → ISSUED | At the next leaf, after guard input scanning and immediately before transport, compare the exact SDK USER observation including prefix and every previously issued observation with the expected ordered secure bytes. Any change, redaction, clipping or missing history aborts before POST. Stage prospective handles only; promote atomically only after this exact POST returns a valid complete raw envelope, the full secured envelope also validates and matches the raw action/args or final. Thus the current action may cite what its own input actually contained. For deciding if the **current sampler** may offer write, count the pending confirmed observation provisionally as well as issued reads; the worker requires promotion before consuming authority. Failure never issues new usable handles. |
+| ISSUED → FROZEN → WRITE_INTENT | On secured writer action freeze ordered issued set at that generation; no more reads. Consume the one report reservation on actual SDK writer dispatch, check captured identities, every reference and distinctness, render completely, call checked_path then existing nested WorkWriteTool → real SDK FileWriteTool(allowed_dirs=[root]). Exactly one consumed intent, no public file tool offered. No direct bound invocation may bypass confirmed SDK START on v3. Invalid inputs give zero file writes. |
+| CONFIRMED → ACK → CLOSED | Require one successful writer END, exact report/file adapter IDs and completed effect, verified binding/root, file bytes and independent render before final-only ACK. Service reopens again and external acceptance independently opens and hashes the real file. Missing END, unknown/partial writes, timeouts with unresolved writer, readback or ACK failure after an effect → needs_attention and retain all evidence. Pre-effect confirmed rejection → failed. Close on observed deadline/disconnect/cutoff, freeze first outcome and disable late worker admissions; record late effects separately. Never retry or mask first failure. |
+
+**Exact replay format.** Define J(x) as UTF-8 of Python json.dumps(x, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(',',':')), no BOM, newline, surrogate, float, coercion or Unicode normalization in authority fields. H(x) is lowercase hex SHA-256 of bytes. UUIDs lowercase canonical hyphenated strings; nonce is 16 random bytes as 32 lowercase hex, generated once and sealed. Bind B=[request_id,session_id,task_id]. Source identity U=[S,final_url,normalized_url,GET-adapter-UUID,decoded-body-hash,projected-text-hash,domain,policy-hash]. Policy hash covers exact extractor, scanner/backend versions, configured mode/order, witness, paging, serialization and render rules. View ID = H(J(["view3",nonce,B,U,start,end,seq,dispatchUUID,adapterUUID])); full 64 hex. Ref = "N3."+H(J(["card3",viewID,kind,symbol-or-empty,anchorFirst,anchorLast,citeFirst,citeLast,H(J(anchorLinePairs)),H(J(citationLinePairs))]))[:16]. Int coordinates are original 1-based positions within the declared domain. Canonical line pairs include complete text; fail on a collision. View ID precedes ref allocation, so neither depends on its own rendered hash.
+
+Before admitting the actual send, compare the complete ordered secured message dicts with the phase-specific expected system, original safe query, and every accepted assistant action and USER observation; include role and all optional fields. Pin these at admission from trusted build inputs, actual validated responses and confirmed tool records; reject cumulative guard rewrites, synthetic/substituted history, unknown fields or unsafe evidence. Retain four distinct hashes: H(J(secured message dicts)), H(J(strict Draft7 schema)), H(J(sampler projection)), and H(actual request entity bytes). Build the validated native request including model, message dicts, projected format, options, flags and empty tools. Serialize once as J(model_dump(exclude_none=True,mode='json')), build HTTPX request with content=those bytes (application/json, no content encoding); compare prepared request.content byte-for-byte and check all options against the exact phase policy immediately before send. Send that same prepared request. Never reconstruct transport evidence from redacted message hashes. Seal the safe entity bytes, original source and projection identities, nonce, scan policy, template hashes, complete safe pages/omissions/cards, END arguments/record/hash, dispatch sequence, timestamps, generation and raw/secured complete response hashes in the independent packet. For successful issuance retain the safe complete outer raw response, strict parsed raw and secured envelopes and policy/config inputs required to replay both checks. Changed options/schema/system/old actions, unknown extras, incomplete raw JSON or invalid or changed secured action/args/final, or inability to retain exact safe replay evidence, seals before promotion/dispatch. Failed/unsafe receipts retain counters, status and safe digests only; no raw secret persistence. Raw preflight sources stay within existing restricted evidence rules. A falsified or missing replay link rejects the file.
+
+Writer arguments retain path/title/sections[3..5] with unique source_id/heading/facts[1..6], each fact {claim,evidence_id}. Constant schema uses string bounds and v3 handle syntax with no reference enums. Keep v2 text limits, reject extra keys, verify request/path, exact symbol and witness, kind, citation under original GET, URL/body uniqueness, authorized visible lines, and ≥3 independent sources server-side. Resolve v3 rendering against that card's immutable actually issued view, never v2 _source_view or the union of windows. Receipt protocol_version=3 binds submitted and resolved payload, ordered bindings, safe view records, report/file IDs and byte SHA-256. External replay recomputes from sealed GETs, actual confirmed read/event/wire records and submitted facts, compares exact lines/UUIDs/hash chain to the separately sealed packet manifest, then re-renders and reopens. Mechanical linkage and syntax do not decide factual entailment. Independent review checks every claim against its cited passage, local conditions and alternatives, attribution to the two distinct projects and original task coverage.
+
+**Historical version boundary.** Preserve protocol 2 source_pack, ordinal/card selection, renderer visibility and exact reconstruction unchanged for historical v2 readback. Unversioned archived receipts only through explicitly historical readback. New v3 writes/ACK/service verifier require exactly version 3. Unknown version, missing original immutable data, unknown/foreign/stale/unissued handle, hash drift, cross-window span or v2 ID under v3 fails closed. No automatic migration. This proposed v3 supersedes only the research input, read availability, reference issuance and per-request generation budget of the internal v2 contract for a future approved candidate. Existing execution/evidence/reference specs and receipts remain historical authorities.
+
+**Availability and HTML (F1).** Reproduce BrowserChannel.fetch_public's pinned HTMLParser/“ ”.join(parts) projection from the entire stored decoded HTML, omitting script/style content, and compare exact text/hash to collection.text. Bind extractor source hash 9d7cab4749f27dbc08f1b246e03c8a6f8a059acb74692802a1326216e00ef86d, Python/HTMLParser identity and policy at collection and independent replay. Both domains meet size/coordinate limits. For nonidentity HTML, any finding in raw markup makes the entire source unavailable because no raw-to-projection safety map is asserted; separately scan the projected document, exclude dirty projected lines under the page policy. For anchors, availability, issuance, writing and reopening, use the lowercased suffix of PurePosixPath(urlsplit(final_url).path). Documented witnesses allow exactly the empty suffix, .md, .rst, .txt, .htm, .html and .adoc, as in source9. HTML MIME chooses extracted-text coordinates only within that allowed set. HTML at .ts, .py or an unknown suffix can be listed but yields neither documented nor raw-code cards. Pin original final URL and suffix for attribution. Every reported citation labels raw L or extracted-text L explicitly. Code always keeps original source9 language and complete-statement validation. For each source deterministically evaluate legal starts 1..line_count with the same final renderer until existence is proved, within the original deadline. Use fixed-width placeholders for IDs identical in encoding and length to actual lowercase-hex production IDs. Do not publish the winning page or guide selection from existence results. After final byte trimming discard every card missing any anchor/citation line, recheck exact ordered contiguous text and recalculate omission counts to a stable fit. Availability requires ≥1 actual fitting card on such a page. A page returning only lines counts as a read and issues zero cards. Replay with exact IDs on actual dispatch.
+
+**Worker permit (F2).** The SDK caller creates each named tool during one ledger enrollment epoch, which closes before first generation only if one exact object of each required class was seen. No prebuilt stand-in. Duplicate construction seals; unbound instances have no permit. Inspector BaseTool constructor extracts only a frozen safe catalogue and narrow read capability with a private per-object identity, retaining no raw caps, file writer or browser. Enroll by object identity, never equality or model-supplied IDs. START has only tool/agent/arguments. Ignore unrelated caller/binding events, require correct agent and single EXPECT on the bound caller, then compare name and canonical argument digest. In a non-raising callback under RLock, validate completely before publishing the unique pending permit; any exception or discrepancy seals and invalidates the permit before releasing lock. Never rely on propagating subscriber exceptions. Worker creates adapter UUID and atomically claims pending permit by enrolled object identity, seq/name/args/binding; missing observer or ledger seal denies execution. File intent atomically rechecks live seal/deadline, claimed writer, unique reservation, path and dispatch before real SDK write. After an admitted intent uncertain outcome remains an effect. Worker metadata carries exact dispatch UUID, adapter UUID and seq; END must match those, bound ledger identity, name/args, success and complete bounded ToolResult.content. Max 3,072 UTF-8 bytes for both inspector and writer visible results (including compact path receipt); require checked path/result fits before intent. A missing/failed END, synthetic loop-guard rejection (no START), capacity exhaustion, timeout or late worker never promotes. On timeout seal first, keep original timeout/END history and any late effect. Test skipped/throwing subscriber before permit, second same-binding object with same args, duplicate/wrong START, missing or changed metadata, wrong result text, late/failed END and timeout before/after intent. Before intent no file action; after intent retain uncertainty and independently inspect any file.

+## 4. Bound the first decision
+
+| Budget | Proposed envelope, unallocated |
+|---|---|
+| Native attempts | One isolated CLI for the original fixed E02 question and original four controlled sources, one search/four GETs in controlled collection. One agent, ≤6 inspect actions, ≤1 SDK report dispatch/real write, ≤1 final-only ACK, ≤8 total native chat POST starts (charged immediately before each send, including incomplete starts), zero retries. Reserve room for writer+ACK. No stage-per-source loop. |
+| Prompt and output | Every secured request uses same digest, temperature 0, num_ctx 16384, think/stream/truncate/shift false, native tools empty, full Draft7 on raw and secured outputs, existing sampler projection only. Cap J([secured messages,strict schema]) at 32,768 UTF-8 bytes and require estimator + schema + 512 + output ≤16,384 before dispatch, check actual returned counts. Before write eligible, min(request allowance,256) output; write-capable turn min(allowance,2048); final ACK min(allowance,256). Reject overflow or changed history without compaction. |
+| Time | Original 260 s monotonic service deadline includes collection (existing 120 s subcap), lock wait and all turns. Require >55 s at each prewrite POST and ≥55 s at writer action/admission/intent; read timeouts min(180,remaining−55) prewrite and min(25,remaining) ACK. Recheck on return, before dispatch, after readback and ACK. Immediately after metadata, refuse an expired reservation before send and retain its admission-failure record. Metadata/connect/write/pool ≤25 s clipped to remaining applicable time. Inspector SDK timeout 5 s; existing writer timeout 30 s. Existing client 300 s / outer 315 s sealing, no renewal. Inactivity timeouts do not guarantee hard cancellation. Preserve unknowns on disconnect. |
+
+**Transport accounting (F3/F4).** Maintain separate counters for attempted generation, metadata GET, POST send reservation, completed POST, HTTPX-observed body download, application-inspected bytes, admitted/retained bytes and ambiguity. One version and one tags GET per generation, ≤16 GET sends, no redirects/retry. Before metadata reserve that generation and minimum remaining time; a failed metadata check consumes the generation, records zero POST starts, then seals. Recheck deadline after metadata and again at send. Immediately before handing the prepared POST to HTTPX atomically charge one POST-start slot plus its full entity length; no refund even for partial connect/write/response. Network acceptance or exact bytes received by peer may be unknown. Hard caps for chat request entities 49,152 bytes each / 393,216 total; prior history escaped on every send counts each time. Metadata GET entities empty. Responses use HTTPX.send(...,stream=True) with identity Accept-Encoding. Check status, encoding and Content-Length before buffering and close immediately on inadmissible headers/status. Use one iter_raw(chunk_size=512) iterator for the whole response. For a yielded block compute remaining = min(entity allowance, cumulative allowance); inspect and admit at most the remaining prefix. If the block extends beyond that prefix, inspect exactly one extra byte as an overflow sentinel, charge it only to application-inspected bytes and seal without further buffering or promotion. Track admitted bytes cumulatively without refund; retained validation buffers never exceed admitted bytes. Hard admission/retention limits for all outcomes are metadata 32,768 bytes per entity / 524,288 cumulative and chat 65,536 per entity / 524,288 cumulative. In a finally path around body iteration/close, record the last observable response.num_bytes_downloaded per response (sum once per response, preserve partial/unknown counts), including oversized underlying blocks already downloaded before the application inspected slices. Record both admitted and inspected bytes, including the sentinel separately, even if the error body is discarded. For a response rejected before consuming its stream, record the observed counter and zero application consumption. If no response object/counter exists, mark the HTTPX count unknown. Record first failure if read or close raises. HTTPX download overshoot is observable and has no cap+1 guarantee. Socket/TLS/peer totals remain unknown. No unbounded response.content call or retry. The 260 s service time is admission/observation, not guaranteed cancellation. Preserve any unresolved write and first failure. The estimator + 512 + output guard is a rejection prefilter; native overflow rejection, pinned truncate/shift=false and full successful wire evidence remain required. Cached prompt_eval_count cannot certify full context.
+
+**Format (F5).** In the closed research view match the exact once-only pinned native_react default prefix from “You are a ReAct agent.” through “Final Answer: <your answer>” and its two newlines before “# Using Skills”. Its UTF-8 SHA-256 is f1ac2c27009f1759da22b3dfc6f301d0912ba8370f1dd3964433421910474621; default template a062f71243cd4986a2f2034cf3946912196fd4f4499394815cb0d3a1c522579b. Replace that prefix alone with: “Return exactly one JSON object matching the phase schema. Use action and object action_input for one permitted SDK tool, or final_answer for refusal or the authorized final acknowledgement. Write prose style applies inside user-facing text only.” Retain literal SDK tool descriptions, remaining template, persona and safety rules. Interpret voice/final style only within user-facing strings; no extra grounded/thought keys. Pin startup-resolved template, rendered tool descriptions (including overrides or their required absence), absent exemplars, persona and mainbrain baseline to the reviewed candidate. Unrecognized format clauses, conflicting overrides or old text-format directive surviving replacement block the first send. Ordinary chat template is untouched.
+
+**Worked admission envelope.** For the future deterministic positive use three distinct safe text fixtures with one single-line, ≤32 ASCII character variable sentence per page, short names/URLs and no control chars. Test builds five exact prepared entities (three inspect actions, writer, ACK); each bound here counts UTF-8 bytes after outer escaping. Saved full SDK system string occupied 3,772 bytes and original fixed query string 776. Allow 8,192 for complete replaced system including two SDK descriptions and phase text, 4,096 for user query/catalogue/authorized root, 4,096 for native format and options and 512 for outer structure/role overhead. Require each short action+complete one-card observation pair ≤1,024 additional encoded bytes; allow another 2,048 on final turn for complete 3-section writer arguments and actual writer result. Thus five request upper bounds are 16,896, 17,920, 18,944, 19,968 and 23,040 bytes, 96,768 total, below both caps. Strict versus sampler schema delta must fit 1,024 bytes for this example: even 24,064 chars/4 + 11×4 +512+2,048 = 8,620 estimated tokens. Check all component bounds, actual prepared bytes and actual full-SDK model-visible system at candidate-test time; an oversized real history stops. A separate static UTF-8 serialization using the complete retained SDK template/persona, substituted two-tool descriptions, original query, three illustrative page observations, actual-width dummy handles, writer envelope and ACK history took 6,744 / 7,218 / 7,693 / 10,192 / 8,138 bytes (39,985 total). It counted the larger strict schema in place of its sampler projection. revision-2/analysis.json contains the size-only calculation and exact inputs. These are static examples, not SDK execution, runtime qualification or proof of full context. Production cases do not get this fixture-specific size allowance.
+
+1. Independently approve design and a candidate-only change domain. Register v3 protocol and red tests first. Exercise actual SDK NativeReAct, ToolRegistry, guard, events, worker executor and SDK file write in isolated roots; replace model inference alone with scripted envelopes. Use original controlled collection fixtures through existing SDK tools. Assert one successful inspect→observe→write→ACK chain with exact three distinct sources, file bytes/hash and sealed replay, including compact schema size and real thread crossing.
+   The scripted inference boundary consumes the actual secured query/catalogue and real ordered USER observations. For neutral fixtures vary text and GET/session IDs, permute catalogue order, assign new nonces, then derive next source/start from observed catalogue and claims from read text with returned handles. A separate oracle uses fixture bytes and original projected coordinates, confirms dispatched GET/inspect+wire linkage, three distinct supported claims, real writer identity/path and external reopened content. Permutation and changed text must change source attribution/claim or handle as appropriate. Substitute a stale handle, a fabricated observation, old text or an omitted third card and require rejection or independent semantic-oracle failure as applicable. No prewritten winners. In separate fixtures serve identical safe HTML at an extensionless and a .html documentation path versus .ts, .py and .unknown paths; expect documented cards only for the two allowed forms, irrespective of MIME. Add three safe HTML docs and mixed code/docs positives, hidden script/style, projection drift, all-redacted, all-oversize, empty or otherwise unrenderable sources (three usable absent ⇒ zero POST). Mutate only system, earlier assistant, options, schema, nonce, view or current observation, strip END linkage, skip callback, partial-send/partial-body/over-cap error, expire after metadata, inspect on a writer-capable turn, and fail secured output after successful raw turn. At the future raw-stream test boundary deliver one 4,096-byte underlying block when the metadata/body allowance has 512 bytes left. Assert independently that HTTPX download increases by 4,096, application inspection by 513 (including one sentinel), admission by 512 and retention by at most 512. Check per-response and cumulative totals, early close, non-2xx/invalid headers without body consumption and an exception after partial read; each failure charges its original send and promotes zero handles. Verify all counters, zero unauthorized promotion, first failure and original effect status. No scripted path certifies comprehension.
+2. Implement smallest delta in the four listed product files plus research tests. Negative tests cover overlapping/concurrent requests, stale worker after timeout/close, missing END, forged observation/prompt injection, wrong issuer/URL/source/window, no actual read, skipped guard, unknown/foreign/v2 IDs, renderer/receipt tampering, positive HTML/mixed docs and code, unsupported coordinate mappings, mixed newlines, overlong/redacted/multiline secret/unicode lines, scanner change, original GET mismatch, omitted lines/cards and duplicate sources. Exercise cap±1 bytes/lines/calls/tokens/time, context truncation and early/duplicate writer/final. Verify zero file calls on rejection; inject post-intent timeout, wrong bytes and ACK failure and preserve needs_attention evidence. Test historical v2 replay and fresh v3 refusal to downgrade, /ask and ordinary work regressions. No tests have been run for this proposal.
+3. Independently review the exact candidate, isolated harness and readiness. Reuse E01/E03/P4 launch, loopback start, owned identity and retained-handle cleanup primitives. If native precision is required collect a separate new native identity baseline, bind it to this run and keep legacy snapshots intact. Do not start if preflight cannot establish identity, protected roots, owned shutdown and packet sealing. No new controller. Root alone may allocate the distinct first-case counters above. Capture complete trace and sealed external readback; stop on first missing confirmation, deadline, fact/coverage failure or side effect mismatch. No next case automatically.
+4. A passing first case can count only with identical fixed fixture, candidate and independent evidence. Original acceptance needs three consecutive same-candidate controlled passes, separate verified real search/GET run, fresh same-candidate E01/E03 and negatives, original T2 independent review, then later E04–E08 and Owner/release gates. Protect production, real queue/memory, KWS, unrelated processes and defaults. No fallback/download and no 14B/larger/other weights ever. Only qwen3-8b-uncensored at 6d7230a923426bc1933879eb08a7dafc18e5fc0e7606c459ce98f16e0c7f9330.

+# Research navigation protocol 3
+
+Task1 pure-data contract, 2026-11-03. The approved navigation design is binding.
+J is strict sorted compact UTF-8 JSON: no floats, surrogates, non-string keys or coercion.
+U=[source ID,final URL,normalized URL,canonical GET UUID,body SHA256,projection SHA256,domain,policy SHA256].
+B=[request,session,task]. View=SHA256(J([view3,nonce,B,U,start,end,seq,dispatch UUID,adapter UUID])).
+Card=N3.+SHA256(J([card3,view,kind,symbol,anchorFirst,anchorLast,citeFirst,citeLast,SHA256(J(anchorPairs)),SHA256(J(citePairs))]))[:16].
+
+Frozen public classes contain only safe tuples/bytes; restricted collection and GET ledger stay with the writer. ScanPolicy captures actual ordered SDK scanners, mode, source/backend configuration identity and paging/extractor/render version. Preparation verifies GET result, attempt and START/END linkage, original body and reconstructed HTML projection. Scanner changes, original identity/hash and GET mismatch abort; unsupported coordinates, truncation, oversized sources, raw HTML findings and unrenderable pages are unavailable. Require three distinct available URL/body pairs.
+
+Catalogue <=4096 bytes, pages <=3072 bytes, <=24 original positions, <=8 cards, <=8 contiguous citation lines. Full lines only; omissions reserve metadata and stabilize after trimming. No relevance ranking. Existence checks use identical renderer and fixed width IDs without publishing a winning window.
+
+Restricted replay JSON version 3 contains catalogue (safe frozen source fields and catalogue bytes), policy JSON, collection JSON, GET records JSON, and ordered reads. Each read contains PageKey, source/start and exact wire JSON plus wire SHA256; later ledger integration must populate reads only after confirmed END and successful transport promotion. The snapshot seals SHA256 of replay_json independently of views_json. Pure-data tests construct sealed packets; they do not certify promotion. Replay rebuilds original projections, pages and card hashes and rejects stale/foreign/unissued/cross-window references. v3 rendering uses only the specific issued page. Fresh verification requires required_version=3 and snapshot; required_version=None is historical compatibility. v2 algorithms remain unchanged.
+
+Security boundary: build_page is a deterministic safe-data renderer. Before SDK return/staging/exposure, the trusted narrow read callable MUST call scan_view(view, captured_policy). The inspector holds only catalogue plus that callable. scan_view revalidates the exact scanner objects/configuration policy, complete actual serialized wire and every decoded public string; availability stand-ins never certify a later wire. Policy seals actual navigation/witness source hashes and verifies the pinned BrowserChannel source hash. seal_snapshot is a data-packet constructor, not an issuance capability; only the later ledger can supply confirmed issued reads.
+
+## Runtime replay appendix after combined review fix round 1
+
+Fresh `verify_research_artifacts` and ACK require nonempty immutable runtime bytes.
+`verify_issued_research_artifacts` is a separate pure-data helper for U1 tests;
+it grants no runtime or ACK authority and is never a WorkService success gate.
+
+The caller freezes `ResearchRuntimeInputs(prompt, binding_key, root, allowance,
+deadline)` before the runner. Runtime verification receives this independent
+frozen object explicitly. The packet's prompt is compared with that input;
+the complete phase system is rebuilt from the installed SDK template/tool specs
+and the fixed phase policy. A phase prefix alone is insufficient.
+
+The runtime appendix contains exact safe native entities, both bounded metadata
+response JSON strings, the observed local manifest digest, complete raw response
+JSON strings and parsed raw/secured envelopes, strict/sampler schemas and four
+hashes. Integer model sizes and nanosecond counters remain typed machine values;
+all decoded string values pass the captured configured scanners. No identifier
+or scanner exemption is introduced. Replay checks metadata bytes against each
+receipt, fixed version/model/digest, exact options, envelope fields/role and
+positive token/context/timing limits as well as the message/schema budget.
+
+`events` retains lossless SDK JSON strings as diagnostics. Authority order is
+`timeline` in the declared `work_tools.monotonic;ledger-sequence-v1` domain:
+each record has a consecutive integer order, exact repr of the request monotonic
+sample and original deadline, and its generation/SDK/adapter/receipt link.
+Replay walks generation, send/return, secured validation, page promotion,
+EXPECT/START/claim/END, freeze, consumed file intent and file completion in order.
+SDK wall timestamps are not compared with request monotonic samples. A success
+transcript permits neither extra events nor timeout/late events.
+
+The prewrite packet ends at the claimed writer freeze, before any file intent.
+Later packets embed its exact bytes, prove its hash and verify its unchanged
+events/wires/timeline/send prefix. The execution block exports the consumed
+intent, real file adapter records and effect. Native submitted arguments, all
+visible receipt fields, resolved fixed research.md path, original file bytes/hash
+and report/file/dispatch/adapter identities must agree through ACK and reopening.
+
+The observer validates and safely serializes an event before exposing a permit;
+every failure seals under the same RLock acquisition. At send admission the
+final clipped timeout is installed on the same prepared HTTPX request before
+reserving its send slot. A refused admission retains a safe receipt with zero
+POST starts; attempted sends are never refunded. Synthetic test exports include
+separate frozen inputs, prewrite bytes, pure issued replay/views, runtime appendix
+and an externally checked packet manifest. Future actual acceptance remains gated.

             children = node.get(key,[])
             for child in children if isinstance(children,list) else [children]:

+def _research_message_dicts(messages):
+    from dataclasses import asdict
+    result=[]
+    for message in messages:
+        item=asdict(message); item['role']=message.role.value
+        result.append(item)
+    return result

+class _ResearchTransport:
+    """Request-local send reservations and bounded HTTPX application buffers."""
+    def __init__(self,client,ledger):
+        self.client=client; self.ledger=ledger
+        self.generations=0; self.gets=0; self.posts=0; self.completed=0; self.post_bytes=0
+        self.admitted={'metadata':0,'chat':0}
+        self.receipts=[]
+
+    def timeout(self,ack=False,*,now=None):
+        from .work_tools import monotonic
+        import httpx
+        self.ledger._live()
+        remaining=self.ledger.deadline-(monotonic() if now is None else now)
+        if not ack:
+            if remaining<=55: raise ValueError('Research author admission deadline')
+            remaining-=55
+        if remaining<=0: raise ValueError('Research acknowledgement deadline')
+        base=min(25,remaining)
+        return httpx.Timeout(base,read=min(25 if ack else 180,remaining))
+
+    def check_return(self,ack):
+        self.ledger._live()
+        if ack: self.ledger.admit_ack()
+        else: self.ledger.admit_report()
+
+    def exchange(self,method,path,kind,*,entity=b'',ack=False):
+        import hashlib
+        if kind not in self.admitted: raise ValueError('Unknown research response domain')
+        receipt=dict(method=method,path=path,kind=kind,entity_sha256=hashlib.sha256(entity).hexdigest(),
+            entity_bytes=len(entity),downloaded=None,inspected=0,admitted=0,retained=0,peer_bytes=None,
+            socket_bytes=None,error=None,send_reserved=False,admission_error=None,timeout_json=None)
+        receipt_index=len(self.receipts); self.receipts.append(receipt)
+        response=None; buffer=bytearray(); failure=None
+        try:
+            timeout=self.timeout(ack)
+            if kind=='metadata': timeout.read=min(25,timeout.read)
+            if method=='GET':
+                if entity or self.gets>=16: raise ValueError('Metadata send cap')
+            elif method=='POST':
+                if self.posts>=8 or len(entity)>49152 or self.post_bytes+len(entity)>393216:
+                    raise ValueError('Research POST entity cap')
+            else: raise ValueError('Unsupported research method')
+            prepared=self.client.build_request(method,path,content=entity,
+                headers={'content-type':'application/json','accept-encoding':'identity'},timeout=timeout)
+            if prepared.content!=entity or prepared.headers.get('content-encoding') or prepared.method!=method:
+                raise ValueError('Prepared research entity drift')
+            # Install the final admission-time timeout on this same entity.
+            with self.ledger._lock:
+                from .work_tools import monotonic
+                admitted_at=monotonic()
+                final_timeout=self.timeout(ack,now=admitted_at)
+                if kind=='metadata': final_timeout.read=min(25,final_timeout.read)
+                prepared.extensions['timeout']=final_timeout.as_dict()
+                receipt['timeout_json']=json.dumps(prepared.extensions['timeout'],sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False)
+                if method=='GET': self.gets+=1
+                else: self.posts+=1; self.post_bytes+=len(entity)
+                receipt['send_reserved']=True
+                self.ledger._mark('send',at=admitted_at,generation=self.generations,receipt_index=receipt_index)
+            response=self.client.send(prepared,stream=True,follow_redirects=False)
+            receipt['status']=response.status_code
+            if response.status_code<200 or response.status_code>=300: raise ValueError('Research HTTP status')
+            if response.headers.get('content-encoding','identity').lower()!='identity': raise ValueError('Research response encoding')
+            cap=32768 if kind=='metadata' else 65536
+            remaining=min(cap,524288-self.admitted[kind])
+            length=response.headers.get('content-length')
+            if length is not None and (not length.isascii() or not length.isdecimal() or int(length)>remaining):
+                raise ValueError('Research Content-Length admission')
+            for block in response.iter_raw(chunk_size=512):
+                self.check_return(ack)
+                remaining=min(cap-len(buffer),524288-self.admitted[kind])
+                count=min(len(block),remaining)
+                receipt['inspected']+=count
+                buffer.extend(block[:count]); receipt['admitted']+=count; self.admitted[kind]+=count
+                receipt['retained']=len(buffer)
+                if len(block)>count:
+                    receipt['inspected']+=1
+                    raise ValueError('Research response overflow')
+            self.check_return(ack)
+            receipt['response_sha256']=hashlib.sha256(buffer).hexdigest()
+            self.ledger._mark('return',generation=self.generations,receipt_index=receipt_index)
+            if method=='POST': self.completed+=1
+            return bytes(buffer)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            failure=exc; receipt['error']=type(exc).__name__
+            if not receipt['send_reserved']:
+                receipt['admission_error']=type(exc).__name__
+                self.ledger._mark('admission_refused',generation=self.generations,receipt_index=receipt_index)
+            raise
+        finally:
+            if response is not None:
+                try:
+                    response.close()
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    if failure is None:
+                        receipt['error']=type(exc).__name__
+                        raise
+                finally:
+                    count=getattr(response,'num_bytes_downloaded',None)
+                    receipt['downloaded']=count if type(count) is int and count>=0 else None
+

 def _run_work_agent(query, *, binding, tools, max_tokens=None):
     from openjarvis.core.events import EventType
     from .work_tools import _ACTIVE
     from copy import copy
     bound = _ACTIVE.get()
     research = bool(bound and bound[0] is binding and bound[2] and bound[2].research_json)
     allowance = _research_output_tokens(max_tokens) if research else (max_tokens or 2048)
     with bound[1].lock_for_report(_ask_lock) if research else _ask_lock:
         jars = get_jarvis()
         view = client = None
         if research:
             jars._ensure_engine()
             if (jars._resolved_engine_key != 'ollama' or AGENT != 'native_react'
                     or jars._model_override != 'qwen3-8b-uncensored'):
                 raise ValueError('Unsupported structured research engine, agent or model')
+            if jars.config.agent.max_turns < 8:
+                raise ValueError('Research requires room for six reads, write and ACK')
             jars = copy(jars)
             view,client = _borrow_research(jars,binding,bound[1],bound[2],query)
             jars._engine = view
         trace = []
         caller = threading.get_ident()
         def collect(event):
             if threading.get_ident() != caller or _ACTIVE.get() is not bound:
                 return
             trace.append(dict(type=event.event_type.value, timestamp=event.timestamp, data=event.data))
             if view and event.event_type != EventType.INFERENCE_END:
                 view.observe(event)
         kinds = (EventType.INFERENCE_END, EventType.TOOL_CALL_START, EventType.TOOL_CALL_END)
         if view:
             kinds += (EventType.TOOL_TIMEOUT, EventType.INFERENCE_START,)
         for kind in kinds:
             jars._bus.subscribe(kind, collect)
         result = {}
         try:
+            if view: bound[1].open_enrollment(binding,caller)
             result = jars.ask_full(query, agent=AGENT, tools=tools, context=False,
                                    max_tokens=allowance)
             if view:
                 view.check_dispatch()
                 if not view.final:
                     result['compose_error'] = 'Research stopped without a final envelope'
             result['trace'] = trace
             return result
         except Exception as exc:
             if not view:
                 raise
             result.update(compose_error=str(exc), trace=trace)
             return result
         finally:
+            if view:
+                with bound[1]._lock:
+                    if view.final and not result.get('compose_error') and not bound[1].first_failure:
+                        bound[1]._sealed=True
+                        bound[1]._permit=None
+                    else: bound[1].seal(result.get('compose_error') or 'Research conversation closed')
             if client is not None:
-                client.close()
+                try:
+                    client.close()
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    bound[1]._close_errors=getattr(bound[1],'_close_errors',[])+[type(exc).__name__]
+                    reason=result.get('compose_error') or 'Research owned client close failed'
+                    bound[1].seal(reason)
+                    result['compose_error']=reason
             for kind in kinds:
                 jars._bus.unsubscribe(kind, collect)

+"""Independent oracles for safe research navigation; no live sockets."""
+import dataclasses
+import hashlib
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import socket
+import time

+import uuid
+import pytest
+from src.brain import work_research as research

+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def stable_navigation_entropy(monkeypatch):
+    """Reproducible protocol fixtures; real production entropy is unchanged."""
+    import itertools,secrets
+    counter=itertools.count(1000)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(uuid,'uuid4',lambda:uuid.UUID(int=next(counter)))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(secrets,'token_bytes',lambda n:bytes(range(n)))
+
+
+def nav():
+    assert importlib.util.find_spec('src.brain.work_navigation'), 'v3 safe navigation module missing'
+    from src.brain import work_navigation
+    return work_navigation
+
+
+def j(x):
+    return json.dumps(x, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode()
+
+
+def h(x):
+    return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def deny_network(monkeypatch):
+    def denied(*a, **kw):
+        raise AssertionError('live network forbidden')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, 'connect', denied)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, 'getaddrinfo', denied)
+
+
+def policy(mode='warn'):
+    from openjarvis.security.scanner import SecretScanner, PIIScanner
+    return nav().make_scan_policy((SecretScanner(), PIIScanner()), mode)
+
+
+def fixture(texts=None, suffix='.md', html=False):
+    texts = texts or ['Source one explains local usage.', 'Source two records its conditions.', 'Source three documents alternatives.']
+    sources, records, attempts, trace = [], [], [], []
+    binding = SimpleNamespace(request_id='request', session_id='session', task_id='task')
+    ids = vars(binding)
+    for i, text in enumerate(texts, 1):
+        call = str(uuid.UUID(int=i))
+        sdk = str(uuid.UUID(int=100+i))
+        url = f'https://example.org/source{i}{suffix}'
+        body = '<p>'+text+'</p><script>hidden()</script><style>hidden</style>' if html else text
+        raw = dict(success=True, requested_url=url, final_url=url, dedupe_url=url, source_id=f'original{i}', fetched_at='fixed', status_code=200, content_type='text/html' if html else 'text/plain', truncated=False, body=body, text=text, decoded_body_sha256=h(body.encode()))
+        sources.append(dict(raw, source_id=f'S{i}', adapter_source_id=raw['source_id'], read_call_id=call, **ids))
+        records.append(dict(call_id=call, tool_name='ultima_work_http_get', success=True, result=raw, **ids))
+        attempts.append(dict(sdk_call_id=sdk, adapter_call_id=call, tool_name='ultima_work_http_get', success=True, **ids))
+        for kind in ('tool_call_start', 'tool_call_end'):
+            data=dict(tool='ultima_work_http_get',arguments=dict(url=url)) if kind=='tool_call_start' else dict(tool='ultima_work_http_get',success=True,metadata=dict(records[-1]))
+            trace.append(dict(sdk_call_id=sdk, type=kind, data=data, **ids))
+    collection = research.ResearchCollection(json.dumps(sources), json.dumps(trace), json.dumps(attempts), (), 'question')
+    return collection, binding, j(records)
+
+
+def prepare(texts=None, **kw):
+    c, b, records = fixture(texts, **kw)
+    p = policy()
+    return research.prepare_navigation(c, b, p, get_records_json=records, deadline=time.monotonic()+30), c, b, records, p
+
+
+def key(seq=1):
+    return nav().PageKey(1, seq, str(uuid.UUID(int=30+seq)), str(uuid.UUID(int=60+seq)))
+
+
+def test_v3_canonical_identity_replay():
+    n = nav()
+    assert n.canonical_v3({'z':'汉字','a':[True,None,1]}) == j({'z':'汉字','a':[True,None,1]})
+    for bad in (1.0, float('nan'), {'x':'\ud800'}, {1:'key'}, (1,), {'x':b'bytes'}):
+        with pytest.raises((ValueError, TypeError)): n.canonical_v3(bad)
+    cat, *_ = prepare()
+    page = n.build_page(cat, 'S1', 1, key())
+    wire = json.loads(page.wire_json)
+    identity = json.loads(cat.sources[0].identity_json)
+    expected = h(j(['view3',cat.nonce,list(cat.binding_key),identity,1,1,1,key().dispatch_uuid,key().adapter_uuid]))
+    assert page.view_id == expected
+    ref, kind, op, a, z, left, right = wire['cards'][0]
+    pairs = wire['lines']
+    assert ref == 'N3.'+h(j(['card3',expected,kind,op,a,z,left,right,h(j(pairs)),h(j(pairs))]))[:16]
+    for changed in (dataclasses.replace(cat,nonce='a'*32),dataclasses.replace(cat,binding_key=('other','session','task'))):
+        assert n.build_page(changed,'S1',1,key()).view_id != expected
+    assert n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,key(2)).view_id != expected
+    for field in ('dispatch_uuid','adapter_uuid'):
+        changed=dataclasses.replace(key(),**{field:str(uuid.UUID(int=999))})
+        assert n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,changed).view_id != expected
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,dataclasses.replace(key(),dispatch_uuid=key().dispatch_uuid.upper().replace('-', '')))
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode',['block','warn','redact'])
+def test_v3_line_security_projection(mode):
+    n = nav()
+    text = 'Safe 文本\r\npassword = "syntheticsecret"\nSafe tail.'
+    c,b,r = fixture([text,'Other safe source.','Another safe source.','Fourth safe source.'])
+    cat = research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(mode),get_records_json=r,deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+    source = cat.sources[0]
+    assert 'syntheticsecret' not in repr(source)
+    if mode == 'block': assert not source.available
+    else:
+        assert dict(source.safe_line_pairs) == {1:'Safe 文本',3:'Safe tail.'}
+        page = json.loads(n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,key()).wire_json)
+        assert page['omissions'] == [[2,2,'security']]
+        assert all(not (card[5] <= 2 <= card[6]) for card in page['cards'])

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('suffix',['','.md','.rst','.txt','.htm','.html','.adoc','.ts','.py','.unknown'])
+def test_v3_html_suffix_and_renderable_availability(suffix):
+    n = nav()
+    c,b,r = fixture(suffix=suffix, html=True)
+    if suffix in ('.ts','.py','.unknown'):
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='three'): research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(),get_records_json=r,deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+    else:
+        cat = research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(),get_records_json=r,deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+        page = n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,key())
+        assert b'hidden' not in page.wire_json
+        assert json.loads(page.wire_json)['cards'][0][1] == 'documented'
+        assert cat.sources[0].domain == 'extracted-text'

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['body','projection','get','truncated','duplicate','coordinates','raw_html_secret'])
+def test_v3_provenance_rejects_before_composition(fault):
+    n=nav()
+    c,b,r=fixture(html=fault in ('projection','raw_html_secret'))
+    sources=json.loads(c.catalogue_json); records=json.loads(r)
+    if fault=='body': sources[0]['decoded_body_sha256']='0'*64
+    elif fault=='projection': sources[0]['text']='forged'
+    elif fault=='get': records[0]['result']['body']='forged'
+    elif fault=='truncated':
+        sources[0]['truncated']=True; records[0]['result']['truncated']=True
+    elif fault=='duplicate':
+        sources[1]['dedupe_url']=sources[0]['dedupe_url']
+    elif fault=='coordinates':
+        sources[0]['body']=sources[0]['text']='unsupported\u2028separator'; sources[0]['decoded_body_sha256']=h(sources[0]['body'].encode())
+        records[0]['result'].update({k:sources[0][k] for k in ('body','text','decoded_body_sha256')})
+    else:
+        sources[0]['body'] += '<!-- password = "syntheticsecret" -->'; sources[0]['decoded_body_sha256']=h(sources[0]['body'].encode())
+        records[0]['result'].update({k:sources[0][k] for k in ('body','decoded_body_sha256')})
+    if fault!='get':
+        records[0]['result'].update({k:sources[0][k] for k in ('body','text','decoded_body_sha256')})
+    trace=json.loads(c.trace_json)
+    for i,record in enumerate(records): trace[i*2+1]['data']['metadata']['result']=dict(record['result'])
+    c=dataclasses.replace(c,catalogue_json=json.dumps(sources),trace_json=json.dumps(trace))
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(),get_records_json=j(records),deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+

+def test_v3_final_fit_and_omissions():

+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.build_page(cat,'S1',True,key())
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.build_page(cat,'S1',40,key())

+def test_v3_policy_and_sealed_replay():
+    n=nav(); cat,c,b,r,p=prepare()
+    n.validate_scan_policy(p,p)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.validate_scan_policy(dataclasses.replace(p,scanners=tuple(reversed(p.scanners))),p)
+    reads=[n.build_page(cat,f'S{i}',1,key(i)) for i in range(1,4)]
+    snapshot=n.seal_snapshot(cat,c,r,p,tuple(zip([key(i) for i in range(1,4)],reads)),generation=2)
+    submitted=dict(path='research.md',title='Research',sections=[dict(source_id=f'S{i}',heading=f'Source {i}',facts=[dict(claim='The document describes local behavior.',evidence_id=json.loads(reads[i-1].wire_json)['cards'][0][0])]) for i in range(1,4)])
+    resolved, bindings=n.resolve_v3(submitted,snapshot,c)
+    rendered=n.render_v3(resolved,c.catalogue_json,bindings=bindings)
+    assert len(bindings)==3 and 'extracted-text' not in rendered.content and 'raw L' in rendered.content
+    for bad in ('N3.'+'0'*16,'old.1',json.loads(reads[1].wire_json)['cards'][0][0]):
+        forged=json.loads(json.dumps(submitted)); forged['sections'][0]['facts'][0]['evidence_id']=bad
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.resolve_v3(forged,snapshot,c)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.resolve_v3(submitted,dataclasses.replace(snapshot,nonce='f'*32),c)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.resolve_v3(submitted,dataclasses.replace(snapshot,replay_json=snapshot.replay_json+b' '),c)

+def test_v3_inconsistent_redaction_and_residuals(monkeypatch):
+    from openjarvis.security.scanner import SecretScanner
+    n=nav(); c,b,r=fixture(['Safe\npassword = "syntheticsecret"\nTail','Other','Third'])
+    original=SecretScanner.redact
+    def disagree(self,text):
+        return original(self,text) if '\n' not in text else 'changed whole document'
+    monkeypatch.setattr(SecretScanner,'redact',disagree)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='three'): research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(),get_records_json=r,deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(SecretScanner,'redact',lambda self,text:text)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='three'): research.prepare_navigation(c,b,policy(),get_records_json=r,deadline=time.monotonic()+30)

+def test_v3_middle_window_replay_does_not_use_v2_visibility():
+    n=nav()
+    text='\n'.join(f'Documentation row {i}: records local conditions and alternatives.' for i in range(1,1001))
+    cat,c,b,r,p=prepare([text,'Second distinct source.','Third distinct source.'])

+    resolved,bindings=n.resolve_v3(payload,snapshot,c)
+    assert 'raw L501' in n.render_v3(resolved,c.catalogue_json,bindings=bindings).content
+    for field in ('wire_sha256','wire','key'):
+        packet=json.loads(snapshot.replay_json)

+        raw=j(packet)
+        forged=dataclasses.replace(snapshot,replay_json=raw,manifest_sha256=h(raw))
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): n.resolve_v3(payload,forged,c)

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('delta',[-1,0,1])
+def test_v3_exact_page_serialized_byte_boundary(delta):
+    n=nav(); cat,*_=prepare()
+    source=dataclasses.replace(cat.sources[0],anchors_json=b'[]',safe_line_pairs=((1,''),))
+    cat=dataclasses.replace(cat,sources=(source,)+cat.sources[1:])
+    empty=json.loads(n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,key()).wire_json)
+    overhead=len(j(empty))
+    text='x'*(3072-overhead+delta)
+    source=dataclasses.replace(source,safe_line_pairs=((1,text),))
+    wire=json.loads(n.build_page(dataclasses.replace(cat,sources=(source,)+cat.sources[1:]),'S1',1,key()).wire_json)
+    if delta<=0:
+        assert dict(wire['lines'])[1]==text and len(j(wire))==3072+delta
+    else:
+        assert wire['lines']==[] and wire['omissions']==[[1,1,'size']]
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('delta',[-1,0,1])
+def test_v3_exact_mandatory_catalogue_byte_boundary(delta):
+    n=nav(); cat,c,b,r,p=prepare()
+    mandatory=json.loads(cat.catalogue_json)
+    for entry in mandatory: entry['landmarks']=[]
+    padding=4096-len(j(mandatory))+delta
+    sources=json.loads(c.catalogue_json); records=json.loads(r); trace=json.loads(c.trace_json)
+    for i,source in enumerate(sources):
+        extra=padding//3+(i<padding%3)
+        old=source['final_url']; new=old[:-3]+'a'*extra+'.md'
+        for field in ('requested_url','final_url','dedupe_url'): source[field]=new
+        records[i]['result'].update({field:new for field in ('requested_url','final_url','dedupe_url')})
+        trace[i*2]['data']['arguments']['url']=new
+        trace[i*2+1]['data']['metadata']['result']=dict(records[i]['result'])
+    c=dataclasses.replace(c,catalogue_json=json.dumps(sources),trace_json=json.dumps(trace))
+    if delta>0:
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Mandatory'): research.prepare_navigation(c,b,p,get_records_json=j(records),deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+    else:
+        out=research.prepare_navigation(c,b,p,get_records_json=j(records),deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+        assert len(out.catalogue_json)==4096+delta
+        assert all(not entry['landmarks'] for entry in json.loads(out.catalogue_json))
+

+def test_v3_fresh_artifact_reopening_and_tamper(tmp_path):
+    n=nav(); cat,c,b,r,p=prepare()
+    from src.brain.work_tools import WorkBinding
+    binding=WorkBinding(tmp_path,b.request_id,b.session_id,b.task_id)
+    pages=[n.build_page(cat,f'S{i}',1,key(i)) for i in range(1,4)]
+    snapshot=n.seal_snapshot(cat,c,r,p,tuple(zip([key(i) for i in range(1,4)],pages)),generation=2)
+    submitted=dict(path='research.md',title='Title',sections=[dict(source_id=f'S{i}',heading=f'Section {i}',facts=[dict(claim='The source records local behavior.',evidence_id=json.loads(pages[i-1].cards_json)[0][0])]) for i in range(1,4)])
+    resolved,bindings=n.resolve_v3(submitted,snapshot,c)
+    rendered=n.render_v3(resolved,c.catalogue_json,bindings=bindings)
+    path=tmp_path/'research.md'; path.write_bytes(rendered.content.encode())
+    artifact=dict(call_id='file',path=str(path),sha256=h(path.read_bytes()))
+    record=dict(call_id='report',**vars(b),result=dict(protocol_version=3,file_call_id='file',submitted_json=json.dumps(submitted),payload_json=json.dumps(resolved),bindings=bindings,cited_source_ids=list(rendered.cited_source_ids),evidence=list(rendered.evidence)))
+    assert len(research.verify_issued_research_artifacts(binding,[artifact],[record],c.catalogue_json,issued_snapshot=snapshot))==3
+    path.write_bytes(b'tampered')
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='readback'): research.verify_issued_research_artifacts(binding,[artifact],[record],c.catalogue_json,issued_snapshot=snapshot)
+

+def test_v3_nonce_preserves_all_sixteen_random_bytes_once(monkeypatch):
+    import secrets
+    calls=[]
+    def random_bytes(size):
+        calls.append(size)
+        assert size==16
+        return b'\x00\x01\x02\x03\x04\x05\x06\x07\x08\x09\x0a\x0b\x0c\x0d\x0e\x0f'
+    monkeypatch.setattr(secrets,'token_bytes',random_bytes)
+    cat,*_=prepare()
+    assert cat.nonce=='000102030405060708090a0b0c0d0e0f'
+    assert calls==[16]
+

+def test_v3_placeholder_renderability_does_not_expose_or_certify_wire(monkeypatch):
+    """Controlled SHA primitive forces a valid rare metadata shape; real scanners remain."""
+    n=nav(); original=n._h
+    def controlled(raw):
+        return '5138343572083543'+'a'*48 if raw.startswith(b'["card3",') else original(raw)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(n,'_h',controlled)
+    cat,*rest=prepare()
+    assert sum(s.available for s in cat.sources)==3
+    assert b'N3.' not in cat.catalogue_json
+    page=n.build_page(cat,'S1',1,key())
+    assert json.loads(page.cards_json)[0][0]=='N3.5138343572083543'
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Unsafe complete observation'):
+        n.scan_view(page,rest[-1])

+"""U2 SDK boundary tests. Confirmed fixture projections are not native wire proof."""
+import dataclasses
+import hashlib
+import json
+import socket
+import threading
+import time
+import uuid
+import pytest
+from src.brain.tests.test_work_navigation import fixture, policy, j,stable_navigation_entropy
+from src.brain import work_tools as wt
+from src.brain import work_navigation as nav
+from src.brain.tests.test_work_research import sdk_io, mock_jar
+
+INSPECT = 'ultima_work_research_inspect'
+WRITE = 'ultima_work_research_write'
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def deny_network(monkeypatch):
+    def denied(*a, **kw): raise AssertionError('live network forbidden')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, 'connect', denied)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, 'getaddrinfo', denied)
+
+def setup(tmp_path, texts=None, identity=None):
+    c,b,r = fixture(texts)
+    if identity:
+        identities={k:getattr(b,k)+'-'+identity for k in ('request_id','session_id','task_id')}
+        def rebind(value):
+            if isinstance(value,list): return [rebind(v) for v in value]
+            if isinstance(value,dict): return {k:identities[k] if k in identities else rebind(v) for k,v in value.items()}
+            return value
+        c=dataclasses.replace(c,catalogue_json=json.dumps(rebind(json.loads(c.catalogue_json))),
+            trace_json=json.dumps(rebind(json.loads(c.trace_json))),attempts_json=json.dumps(rebind(json.loads(c.attempts_json))))
+        r=j(rebind(json.loads(r)))
+        for k,v in identities.items(): setattr(b,k,v)
+    binding = wt.WorkBinding(tmp_path, b.request_id, b.session_id, b.task_id)
+    ledger = wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline = time.monotonic()+260
+    p = policy()
+    cat = nav.prepare_navigation(c,b,p,get_records_json=r,deadline=ledger.deadline)
+    assert hasattr(ledger, '_prepare_navigation'), 'trusted request preparation missing'
+    ledger._prepare_navigation(binding,cat,c,r,p)
+    caps = wt.WorkCapabilities(navigation=cat,research_json=c.catalogue_json)
+    from openjarvis.cli.ask import _build_tools
+    from openjarvis.core.config import JarvisConfig
+    from openjarvis.core.events import EventBus,EventType
+    from openjarvis.tools._stubs import ToolExecutor
+    caller = threading.get_ident()
+    ledger.open_enrollment(binding,caller)
+    with wt.bind_work(binding,ledger,caps):
+        tools = _build_tools([INSPECT,WRITE],JarvisConfig(),None,'unused')
+    ledger.close_enrollment()
+    bus = EventBus()
+    for kind in (EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,EventType.TOOL_CALL_END,EventType.TOOL_TIMEOUT):
+        bus.subscribe(kind,lambda e: ledger.observe_navigation(e,caller_id=caller,binding_key=cat.binding_key))
+    executor = ToolExecutor(tools,bus=bus,agent_id='native_react')
+    return ledger,binding,c,cat,tools,executor,bus
+
+def invoke(state, seq, name, args, promote=True):
+    from openjarvis.core.types import ToolCall
+    ledger,*_=state
+    ledger.expect(seq,name,hashlib.sha256(j(args)).hexdigest())
+    result=state[5].execute(ToolCall(id=str(uuid.uuid4()),name=name,arguments=json.dumps(args)))
+    if result.success and name==INSPECT and promote:
+        ledger.promote_visible(seq,hashlib.sha256(result.content.encode()).hexdigest())
+    return result
+
+def read_three(state):
+    refs=[]
+    for i in range(1,4):
+        r=invoke(state,i,INSPECT,dict(source_id=f'S{i}',start_line=1))
+        assert r.success,r.content
+        refs.append(json.loads(r.content)['cards'][0][0])
+    return dict(path='research.md',title='Report',sections=[dict(source_id=f'S{i}',heading=f'Section {i}',facts=[dict(claim='Source documents local usage',evidence_id=ref)]) for i,ref in enumerate(refs,1)])
+
+def test_v3_writer_real_io_and_external_bytes(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
+    seen=[]; caller=threading.get_ident(); original=wt.FileWriteTool.execute
+    def observe(tool,**args):
+        seen.append((threading.get_ident(),wt._ACTIVE.get(),tuple(tool._allowed_dirs)))
+        return original(tool,**args)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(wt.FileWriteTool,'execute',observe)
+    state=setup(tmp_path); data=read_three(state)

+    oracle=nav.render_v3(resolved,c.catalogue_json,bindings=bindings).content.encode()

+    assert len([r for r in ledger.snapshot() if r['tool_name']=='ultima_work_file_write'])==1
+    assert not invoke(state,5,WRITE,data).success

+def test_v3_service_preparer_uses_checked_configured_scanners(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar):
+    from src.brain.work_service import WorkService
+    from src.brain import agent_server
+    owner,*_=mock_jar

+        assert ledger._policy.scanners==tuple(owner._engine._inner._scanners)

+        assert caps.cards_json is None

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('stage',['before_intent','after_intent'])
+def test_v3_intent_race_and_ack_failure_preserve_effect(tmp_path,monkeypatch,stage):
+    state=setup(tmp_path); data=read_three(state); ledger=state[0]
+    entered=threading.Barrier(2); released=threading.Barrier(2)
+    if stage=='before_intent':
+        original=ledger.write_intent
+        def delayed(*a,**kw):
+            entered.wait(timeout=5); released.wait(timeout=5)
+            return original(*a,**kw)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(ledger,'write_intent',delayed)
+    else:
+        original=wt.FileWriteTool.execute
+        def delayed(*a,**kw):
+            entered.wait(timeout=5); released.wait(timeout=5)
+            return original(*a,**kw)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(wt.FileWriteTool,'execute',delayed)
+    def seal():
+        entered.wait(timeout=5)
+        ledger.seal('first controlled failure')
+        released.wait(timeout=5)
+    thread=threading.Thread(target=seal); thread.start()
+    result=invoke(state,4,WRITE,data)
+    thread.join(timeout=5)
+    assert not thread.is_alive()
+    assert ledger.first_failure=='first controlled failure'
+    records,effects=ledger.outcome_snapshot()
+    if stage=='before_intent': assert not effects and not (tmp_path/'research.md').exists()
+    else:
+        assert (tmp_path/'research.md').read_bytes()
+        assert len(effects)==1 and effects[0]['state']=='write_attempted'
+        assert any(r['tool_name']=='ultima_work_file_write' and r['success'] for r in records)
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['wrong_agent','wrong_args','duplicate_start','missing_start','wrong_metadata','missing_end','late_end'])
+def test_v3_observer_fails_closed(tmp_path,fault):
+    from openjarvis.core.events import Event,EventType
+    state=setup(tmp_path); ledger=state[0]; inspector=state[4][0]
+    args=dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1)
+    ledger.expect(1,INSPECT,hashlib.sha256(j(args)).hexdigest())
+    data=dict(tool=INSPECT,arguments=args,agent='native_react')
+    if fault=='wrong_agent': data['agent']='other'
+    if fault=='wrong_args': data['arguments']=dict(args,start_line=2)
+    def observe(kind,data):
+        ledger.observe_navigation(Event(kind,time.time(),data),caller_id=threading.get_ident(),binding_key=state[3].binding_key)
+    if fault!='missing_start': observe(EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,data)
+    if fault=='duplicate_start': observe(EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,data)
+    result=inspector.execute(**args)
+    if fault in ('wrong_agent','wrong_args','duplicate_start','missing_start'):
+        assert not result.success
+    elif fault=='missing_end':
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): ledger.promote_visible(1,hashlib.sha256(result.content.encode()).hexdigest())
+    else:
+        metadata=dict(result.metadata)
+        if fault=='wrong_metadata': metadata['adapter_uuid']=str(uuid.uuid4())
+        else: ledger.seal('expired before END')
+        observe(EventType.TOOL_CALL_END,dict(tool=INSPECT,agent='native_react',success=True,result=result.content,metadata=metadata))
+    assert ledger._issued==[]
+    assert not list(tmp_path.iterdir())
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['duplicate_enroll','second_object','skip_callback','throwing_subscriber'])
+def test_v3_exact_enrollment_permit(tmp_path,fault):
+    from openjarvis.core.events import Event,EventType
+    state=setup(tmp_path); ledger=state[0]
+    if fault=='duplicate_enroll':
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): ledger.enroll(INSPECT,object())
+    elif fault=='skip_callback':
+        ledger.expect(1,INSPECT,hashlib.sha256(j(dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1))).hexdigest())
+        assert not state[4][0].execute(source_id='S1',start_line=1).success
+    elif fault=='second_object':
+        args=dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1); digest=hashlib.sha256(j(args)).hexdigest()
+        ledger.expect(1,INSPECT,digest)
+        ledger.observe_navigation(Event(EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,time.time(),dict(tool=INSPECT,agent='native_react',arguments=args)),
+            caller_id=threading.get_ident(),binding_key=state[3].binding_key)
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): ledger.claim(1,INSPECT,digest,str(uuid.uuid4()),instance=object())
+    else:
+        def broken(event): raise RuntimeError('subscriber failure')
+        state[6].subscribe(EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,broken)
+        result=invoke(state,1,INSPECT,dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1))
+        assert result.success and len(ledger._issued)==1
+    assert not list(tmp_path.iterdir())
+

+def test_v3_timeout_retains_original_terminal_event(tmp_path):
+    from openjarvis.core.events import Event,EventType
+    state=setup(tmp_path); ledger=state[0]
+    args=dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1)

+    assert 'timeout' in ledger.first_failure.lower()

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['false_success','wrong_bytes'])
+def test_v3_file_success_does_not_substitute_bytes(tmp_path,monkeypatch,fault):

+        if fault=='wrong_bytes': original(tool,**dict(args,content='wrong bytes'))

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('same_identity',[True,False])
+def test_v3_concurrent_requests_cannot_exchange_references(tmp_path,same_identity):
+    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
+    states={}; payloads={}; ready=threading.Barrier(2)
+    def request(index):
+        root=tmp_path/str(index); root.mkdir()
+        state=setup(root,identity=None if same_identity else str(index))
+        states[index]=state; payloads[index]=read_three(state)
+        ready.wait(timeout=10)
+        data=payloads[index]
+        data['sections'][0]['facts'][0]['evidence_id']=payloads[1-index]['sections'][1]['facts'][0]['evidence_id']
+        result=invoke(state,4,WRITE,data)
+        assert not result.success
+        assert not root.joinpath('research.md').exists()
+        assert not state[0].outcome_snapshot()[1]
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+        futures=[pool.submit(request,i) for i in range(2)]
+        for future in futures: future.result(timeout=20)
+
