+@pytest.mark.parametrize('after_intent',[False,True])
+def test_fix1_f1_timeout_revokes_inside_observer_lock(tmp_path,monkeypatch,after_intent):
+    import concurrent.futures
+    from openjarvis.tools import _stubs
+    state=setup(tmp_path); data=read_three(state); ledger=state[0]
+    entered=threading.Event(); release=threading.Event(); finished=threading.Event()
+    writes=[]; held=[]; actual=[]
+    file_write=wt.FileWriteTool.execute
+    def file(tool,**args):
+        if after_intent: entered.set(); assert release.wait(5)
+        writes.append(args)
+        return file_write(tool,**args)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(wt.FileWriteTool,'execute',file)
+    if not after_intent:
+        intent=ledger.write_intent
+        def delayed(*args,**kw):
+            entered.set(); assert release.wait(5)
+            return intent(*args,**kw)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(ledger,'write_intent',delayed)
+    original_submit=_stubs._TOOL_RUNNER.submit
+    def submit(function,**args):
+        future=original_submit(function,**args)
+        if function.__self__.tool_id!=WRITE: return future
+        actual.append(future)
+        future.add_done_callback(lambda _:finished.set())
+        class Expired:
+            def result(self,timeout):
+                assert entered.wait(5)
+                raise concurrent.futures.TimeoutError()
+            def cancel(self): return False
+        return Expired()
+    monkeypatch.setattr(_stubs._TOOL_RUNNER,'submit',submit)
+    seal=ledger.seal
+    def boundary(reason):
+        if 'timeout' in str(reason).lower():
+            owned=ledger._lock._is_owned(); held.append(owned); release.set()
+            if not owned: assert finished.wait(5)  # Deterministic exposure of the old lock gap.
+        return seal(reason)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(ledger,'seal',boundary)
+    try: result=invoke(state,4,WRITE,data)
+    finally: release.set()
+    actual[0].result(timeout=5)
+    assert held and all(held)
+    assert 'timeout' in ledger.first_failure.lower() and not result.success
+    assert len(writes)==int(after_intent)
+    assert (tmp_path/'research.md').exists()==after_intent
+    if after_intent:
+        assert ledger.outcome_snapshot()[1][0]['state']=='timed_out'
+        assert ledger._late_records
+
+
+def test_fix1_f1_start_serialization_failure_never_exposes_permit(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
+    from openjarvis.core.events import Event,EventType
+    state=setup(tmp_path); ledger=state[0]; seen=[]; seal=ledger.seal
+    args=dict(source_id='S1',start_line=1); ledger.expect(1,INSPECT,hashlib.sha256(j(args)).hexdigest())
+    def inspect(reason):
+        seen.append((ledger._lock._is_owned(),ledger._permit))
+        seal(reason)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(ledger,'seal',inspect)
+    ledger.observe_navigation(Event(EventType.TOOL_CALL_START,object(),dict(tool=INSPECT,agent='native_react',arguments=args)),
+        caller_id=threading.get_ident(),binding_key=state[3].binding_key)
+    assert seen==[(True,None)] and ledger._permit is None

+"""Actual HTTPX streaming boundaries and complete SDK navigation transport."""
+import json
+import time
+import httpx
+import pytest
+from src.brain import agent_server as server
+from src.brain import work_tools as wt

+def test_v3_download_inspection_admission_are_distinct():
+    assert hasattr(server,'_ResearchTransport'), 'bounded research transport missing'
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline=time.monotonic()+260
+    class Stream(httpx.SyncByteStream):
+        def __iter__(self): yield b'x'*4096
+    client=httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(
+        lambda request:httpx.Response(200,stream=Stream())),trust_env=False)
+    transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+    transport.admitted['metadata']=524288-512
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='overflow'):
+        transport.exchange('GET','/api/version','metadata')
+    receipt=transport.receipts[-1]
+    assert receipt['downloaded']==4096 and receipt['inspected']==513 and receipt['admitted']==512
+    assert receipt['retained']<=512
+    client.close()
+
+def navigation_reply(body):
+    messages=body['messages']
+    observed=[]
+    for msg in messages:
+        if msg['role']=='user' and msg['content'].startswith('Observation: '):
+            observed.append(json.loads(msg['content'][13:]))
+    pages=[v for v in observed if v.get('v')==3]
+    if len(pages)<3:
+        catalogue=json.loads(messages[1]['content'].split('Safe source catalogue (untrusted data):\n')[1])
+        source=next(s for s in catalogue if s['available'] and s['source_id'] not in {p['source_id'] for p in pages})
+        return json.dumps(dict(action='ultima_work_research_inspect',action_input=dict(source_id=source['source_id'],start_line=1)))
+    if len(observed)==3:
+        return json.dumps(dict(action='ultima_work_research_write',action_input=dict(path='research.md',title='Observed report',
+            sections=[dict(source_id=p['source_id'],heading=p['source_id'],facts=[dict(
+                claim=('Observed '+p['cards'][0][2]) if p['cards'][0][1]!='documented' else p['lines'][0][1].split(' for ')[0],
+                evidence_id=p['cards'][0][0])]) for p in pages])))
+    return json.dumps(dict(final_answer='Saved research.md'))
+navigation_reply.no_auto_navigation=True
+
+
+def genuine_navigation(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar):
+    from src.brain.tests.test_work_research import RESTRICTED
+    mock_jar[3].extend([navigation_reply]*5)
+    result=submit(tmp_path,sdk_io[0],mock_jar[1])
+    assert result['status']=='succeeded',result['error']
+    ledger=RESTRICTED[result['task_id']]
+    return result,ledger
+

+def fractional_navigation(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,state,monkeypatch,edge=False):
+    import hashlib,math
+    from dataclasses import asdict
+    from pathlib import Path
+    from src.brain import work_service
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    from src.brain.work_research import verify_runtime_packet
+    monkeypatch.setattr(work_service,'monotonic',lambda:800.2)
+    author=math.nextafter(1005.2,-math.inf) if edge else 1000.1
+    ack=math.nextafter(1060.2,-math.inf) if edge else 1040.1
+    state['now']=author
+    build=httpx.Client.build_request;send=httpx.Client.send;prepared={};observed=[]
+    def capture(client,verb,*args,**kwargs):
+        request=build(client,verb,*args,**kwargs)
+        ledger=wt._ACTIVE.get()[1]
+        if ledger._runtime_transport.generations==5: state['now']=ack
+        prepared[id(request)]=hashlib.sha256(request.content).hexdigest()
+        return request
+    def inspect(client,request,*args,**kwargs):
+        digest=hashlib.sha256(request.content).hexdigest()
+        assert prepared.pop(id(request))==digest
+        observed.append(dict(method=request.method,path=request.url.path,at=repr(state['now']),
+            timeout=dict(request.extensions['timeout']),entity_sha256=digest))
+        return send(client,request,*args,**kwargs)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx.Client,'build_request',capture)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx.Client,'send',inspect)
+    from src.brain.tests.test_work_research import RESTRICTED
+    mock_jar[3].extend([navigation_reply]*5)
+    result=submit(tmp_path,sdk_io[0],mock_jar[1]);ledger=RESTRICTED[result['task_id']]
+    if result['status']!='succeeded' and hasattr(ledger,'_runtime_prewrite'):
+        verify_runtime_packet(ledger._binding,ledger.issued_snapshot,ledger._collection,ledger._runtime_prewrite,
+            runtime_inputs=ledger._runtime_inputs)
+    assert result['status']=='succeeded',result['error']
+    assert ledger.deadline==1060.2 and not prepared and len(observed)==15
+    for index,item in enumerate(observed):
+        cap=(1060.2-ack) if index>=12 else (1060.2-author)-55
+        assert item['timeout']==dict(connect=cap,read=cap,write=cap,pool=cap)
+    if not edge: assert observed[0]['timeout']['read']==5.100000000000023
+    verify_runtime_packet(ledger._binding,ledger.issued_snapshot,ledger._collection,ledger._runtime_final,
+        require_ack=True,runtime_inputs=ledger._runtime_inputs)
+    artifact=Path(result['artifacts'][0]['path']);artifact_hash=hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    assert artifact_hash==result['artifacts'][0]['sha256']
+    for name,raw in [('runtime',ledger._runtime_final),('prewrite',ledger._runtime_prewrite),
+            ('issued',ledger.issued_snapshot.replay_json),('views',ledger.issued_snapshot.views_json),
+            ('inputs',canonical_v3(dict(asdict(ledger._runtime_inputs),binding_key=list(ledger._runtime_inputs.binding_key))))]:
+        (tmp_path/('fractional-'+name+'.json')).write_bytes(raw)
+    (tmp_path/'fractional-proof.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(edge=edge,deadline=repr(ledger.deadline),
+        author_at=repr(author),ack_at=repr(ack),prepared_requests=observed,artifact=str(artifact),
+        artifact_sha256=artifact_hash,replay_accepted=True)),encoding='utf8')
+    return ledger
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('ack',[False,True])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('method',['GET','POST'])
+def test_fix2_n1_next_float_above_cap_rejected(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,source9_clock,monkeypatch,ack,method):
+    import math
+    from src.brain.work_research import verify_runtime_packet
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    ledger=fractional_navigation(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,source9_clock,monkeypatch)
+    packet=json.loads(ledger._runtime_final if ack else ledger._runtime_prewrite)
+    index=(12 if ack else 0)+(2 if method=='POST' else 0)
+    receipt=packet['transport']['receipts'][index];timeout=json.loads(receipt['timeout_json'])
+    timeout['read']=math.nextafter(timeout['read'],math.inf)
+    receipt['timeout_json']=json.dumps(timeout,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Runtime final timeout admission drift'):
+        verify_runtime_packet(ledger._binding,ledger.issued_snapshot,ledger._collection,canonical_v3(packet),
+            require_ack=ack,runtime_inputs=ledger._runtime_inputs)

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('ack',[False,True])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('method',['GET','POST'])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('side',[-1,0,1])
+def test_fix2_n1_fractional_send_boundary(monkeypatch,ack,method,side):
+    import math
+    threshold=1060.2 if ack else 1005.2
+    now=math.nextafter(threshold,-math.inf) if side<0 else math.nextafter(threshold,math.inf) if side>0 else threshold
+    monkeypatch.setattr(wt,'monotonic',lambda:now)
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger();ledger.deadline=1060.2;observed=[];prepared=[]
+    def receive(request):
+        assert request is prepared[0]
+        observed.append(dict(request.extensions['timeout']))
+        return httpx.Response(200,stream=httpx.ByteStream(b'{}'))
+    with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(receive),trust_env=False) as client:
+        build=client.build_request
+        def capture(*args,**kwargs):
+            request=build(*args,**kwargs);prepared.append(request);return request
+        monkeypatch.setattr(client,'build_request',capture)
+        transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+        if side<0:
+            assert transport.exchange(method,'/api/chat' if method=='POST' else '/api/version',
+                'chat' if method=='POST' else 'metadata',ack=ack)==b'{}'
+            cap=(1060.2-now)-(0 if ack else 55)
+            assert observed==[dict(connect=cap,read=cap,write=cap,pool=cap)] and cap>0
+        else:
+            with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='admission deadline|acknowledgement deadline'):
+                transport.exchange(method,'/api/chat' if method=='POST' else '/api/version',
+                    'chat' if method=='POST' else 'metadata',ack=ack)
+            assert not observed and not transport.receipts[-1]['send_reserved']
+

+def test_fix2_f3_writer_transcript_identity(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,kind):

+def escaped_scanner_fixture():
+    """Reuse the reviewed synthetic fixture without duplicating decoded evidence."""
+    import ast,inspect,textwrap
+    from src.brain.tests.test_work_navigation import test_v3_placeholder_renderability_does_not_expose_or_certify_wire
+    tree=ast.parse(textwrap.dedent(inspect.getsource(test_v3_placeholder_renderability_does_not_expose_or_certify_wire)))
+    reference=next(n.value for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n,ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value,str)
+        and n.value.startswith('N3.') and len(n.value)==19 and n.value[3:].isdecimal())
+    return reference[3:]
+
+
+def escaped_envelope(value):
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    raw=canonical_v3(value).decode()
+    digits=set(escaped_scanner_fixture())
+    encoded=''.join('\\u'+format(ord(c),'04x') if c in digits else c for c in raw)
+    if json.loads(encoded)!=value: raise AssertionError('Synthetic escape fixture mismatch')
+    return encoded
+
+
+def rewrite_response(packet,index,value,content):
+    import hashlib
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    wire=packet['wires'][index];wire['raw']['message']['content']=content
+    wire['raw_envelope']=value;wire['secured_envelope']=value
+    wire['raw_response']=canonical_v3(wire['raw']).decode()
+    receipt=packet['transport']['receipts'][index*3+2];size=len(wire['raw_response'].encode())
+    receipt.update(response_sha256=hashlib.sha256(wire['raw_response'].encode()).hexdigest(),
+        downloaded=size,inspected=size,admitted=size,retained=size)
+    packet['transport']['admitted']['chat']=sum(r['admitted'] for r in packet['transport']['receipts'] if r['kind']=='chat')
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['empty','whitespace','escaped_final','escaped_action'])
+def test_fix2_f4_decoded_envelope_replay(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,fault):
+    import hashlib
+    from src.brain.work_research import verify_runtime_packet
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    _,ledger=genuine_navigation(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar)
+    action=fault=='escaped_action'
+    raw=ledger._runtime_prewrite if action else ledger._runtime_final
+    verify_runtime_packet(ledger._binding,ledger.issued_snapshot,ledger._collection,raw,require_ack=not action,runtime_inputs=ledger._runtime_inputs)
+    packet=json.loads(raw);original_prefix=packet['prewrite_packet'];index=len(packet['wires'])-1
+    if action:
+        value=packet['wires'][index]['raw_envelope'];value['action_input']['title']=escaped_scanner_fixture()
+        start=json.loads(packet['events'][-1]);start['data']['arguments']=value['action_input'];packet['events'][-1]=json.dumps(start)
+        next(n for n in reversed(packet['timeline']) if n['kind']=='expect')['args_hash']=hashlib.sha256(canonical_v3(value['action_input'])).hexdigest()
+    else: value=dict(final_answer='' if fault=='empty' else ' \t ' if fault=='whitespace' else escaped_scanner_fixture())
+    content=escaped_envelope(value) if fault.startswith('escaped') else canonical_v3(value).decode()
+    if fault.startswith('escaped'):
+        assert all(s.scan(content).clean for s in ledger._policy.scanners)
+        assert any(not s.scan(escaped_scanner_fixture()).clean for s in ledger._policy.scanners)
+    rewrite_response(packet,index,value,content)
+    assert packet['prewrite_packet']==original_prefix
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Unsafe native evidence string' if fault.startswith('escaped') else 'Empty research final answer'):
+        verify_runtime_packet(ledger._binding,ledger.issued_snapshot,ledger._collection,canonical_v3(packet),
+            require_ack=not action,runtime_inputs=ledger._runtime_inputs)
+

+def rewrite_wire(packet,index):
+    import hashlib
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    wire=packet['wires'][index]; request=json.loads(wire['entity']); request['messages']=wire['secured_messages']
+    entity=canonical_v3(request); wire['entity']=entity.decode(); wire['hashes']['messages']=hashlib.sha256(canonical_v3(request['messages'])).hexdigest()
+    wire['hashes']['entity']=hashlib.sha256(entity).hexdigest()
+    receipt=packet['transport']['receipts'][index*3+2]; receipt['entity_sha256']=wire['hashes']['entity']; receipt['entity_bytes']=len(entity)
+    packet['transport']['post_bytes']=sum(len(w['entity'].encode()) for w in packet['wires'])
+

+            retained=len(wire['raw_response'].encode()),inspected=len(wire['raw_response'].encode()),downloaded=len(wire['raw_response'].encode()))

+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('method',['GET','POST'])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('expired',[False,True])
+def test_fix1_f5_final_send_timeout_and_refusal_receipt(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,source9_clock,monkeypatch,method,expired):
+    from src.brain.tests.test_work_research import RESTRICTED
+    state=source9_clock;state['now']=200
+    build=httpx.Client.build_request; changed=[False]
+    def advance(client,verb,*args,**kwargs):
+        request=build(client,verb,*args,**kwargs)
+        if verb==method and not changed[0]: state['now']=206 if expired else 204;changed[0]=True
+        return request
+    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx.Client,'build_request',advance)
+    mock_jar[3].append('{"final_answer":"refused"}')
+    result=submit(tmp_path,sdk_io[0],mock_jar[1]); ledger=RESTRICTED[result['task_id']]; transport=ledger._runtime_transport
+    assert result['status']=='failed' and changed[0]
+    if expired:
+        assert transport.posts==0 and transport.gets==(2 if method=='POST' else 0)
+        receipt=transport.receipts[-1]
+        assert receipt['method']==method and receipt['send_reserved'] is False and receipt['admission_error']
+    else:
+        phase='author' if method=='POST' else 'metadata'
+        assert next(timeout for label,timeout in state['requests'] if label==phase)==dict(connect=1,write=1,pool=1,read=1)

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('variant',['text','permuted','html','mixed'])
+def test_v3_native_react_observation_driven_chain(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,monkeypatch,variant):
+    import hashlib,itertools,secrets,uuid
+    from pathlib import Path
+    from src.brain.work_service import WorkService
+    jar,runner,bodies,replies=mock_jar
+    persona=server._load_mainbrain_system_prompt()
+    assert persona==server.MAINBRAIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT
+    jar.config.agent.system_prompt=persona
+    jar.config.agent.default_system_prompt=persona
+    number=['text','permuted','html','mixed'].index(variant)
+    counter=itertools.count(2000+number*1000)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(uuid,'uuid4',lambda:uuid.UUID(int=next(counter)))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(secrets,'token_bytes',lambda size:bytes([number+1])*size)
+    urls=['https://example.com/alpha.md','https://example.com/beta.html','https://example.com/gamma']
+    lines=['Alpha runs locally.','Beta checks limits.','Gamma lists alternatives.']
+    if variant=='mixed': urls[0]='https://example.com/alpha.py'; lines[0]='queue.dispatch(item)'
+    fixtures=dict(zip(urls,lines))
+    if variant=='permuted': urls=[urls[2],urls[0],urls[1]]
+    def fetch(method,url,**kwargs):
+        text=fixtures[url]
+        body='<p>'+text+'</p><script>hidden()</script><style>hidden</style>' if variant=='html' else text
+        return httpx.Response(200,headers={'content-type':'text/html' if variant=='html' else 'text/plain'},text=body,request=httpx.Request(method,url))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx,'request',fetch)
+    captured={}; original=server._prepare_research_navigation
+    def prepare(collection,*,binding,ledger):
+        captured.update(ledger=ledger,binding=binding,collection=collection)
+        return original(collection,binding=binding,ledger=ledger)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(server,'_prepare_research_navigation',prepare)
+    # This script consumes actual catalogue and observations. The fixture
+    # transport will not invent SDK observations or issue server-side cards.
+    choose=navigation_reply
+    replies.extend([choose]*5)
+    result=WorkService(tmp_path,runner=runner,browser_channel=sdk_io[0],research_preparer=prepare).submit_work(
+        'Research a subject '+variant,request_id='request-'+variant,session_id='session-'+variant,context={'research':True,'source_urls':urls})
+    assert result['status']=='succeeded',(result['error'],[(r.get('tool_name'),r.get('error')) for r in captured['ledger'].snapshot() if r.get('error')])
+    assert len(bodies)==5
+    assert [b['options']['num_predict'] for b in bodies]==[256,256,256,2048,256]
+    from src.brain.work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    for body in bodies:
+        system=body['messages'][0]['content']
+        assert system.count(persona)==1 and system.startswith('Return exactly one JSON object matching the phase schema.')
+        assert '# Using Skills' in system and 'Final Answer: <your answer>' not in system
+    assert result['artifacts'][0]['path'].endswith('research.md')
+    ledger=captured['ledger']
+    strict_sizes=[len(canonical_v3([w['secured_messages'],w['strict_schema']])) for w in ledger._runtime_wires]
+    entity_sizes=[len(w['entity'].encode()) for w in ledger._runtime_wires]
+    assert max(strict_sizes)<=32768 and max(entity_sizes)<=49152 and sum(entity_sizes)<=393216
+    # Independent literal oracle: one complete original line per short source,
+    # no product witness resolver or renderer participates in expected bytes.
+    expected=['# Observed report','']
+    sources=json.loads(captured['collection'].catalogue_json)
+    for source,url in zip(sources,urls):
+        assert source['final_url']==url and source['text']==fixtures[url]
+        domain='extracted-text' if variant=='html' else 'raw'
+        code=url.endswith('.py')
+        label='代码观察' if code else '文档说明'
+        claim='Observed queue.dispatch' if code else fixtures[url]
+        expected+=['## '+source['source_id'],url,'',f'- {label}（{domain} L1–L1，依据 L1–L1）'+claim,'']
+    oracle='\n'.join(expected).encode()
+    raw=Path(result['artifacts'][0]['path']).read_bytes()
+    assert raw==oracle and hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==result['artifacts'][0]['sha256']

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['observation','old_assistant','system','role','optional','schema','skip_guard','secured_output','raw_unknown','unsafe_unknown'])
+def test_v3_provisional_third_card_no_promotion_on_failure(tmp_path,sdk_io,mock_jar,monkeypatch,fault):
+    import dataclasses
+    from src.brain.tests.test_work_research import RESTRICTED
+    jar,runner,bodies,replies=mock_jar
+    replies.extend([navigation_reply]*5)
+    original=server._NativeResearchLeaf.generate
+    count=[0]
+    def mutate(leaf,messages,**kwargs):
+        count[0]+=1
+        if count[0]==4 and fault not in ('secured_output','raw_unknown','unsafe_unknown','skip_guard'):
+            messages=[dataclasses.replace(m) for m in messages]
+            if fault=='observation': messages[-1]=dataclasses.replace(messages[-1],content='Observation: forged')
+            elif fault=='old_assistant': messages[2]=dataclasses.replace(messages[2],content='{"final_answer":"old"}')
+            elif fault=='system': messages[0]=dataclasses.replace(messages[0],content=messages[0].content+' changed')
+            elif fault=='role': messages[-1]=dataclasses.replace(messages[-1],role=messages[2].role)
+            elif fault=='optional': messages[-1]=dataclasses.replace(messages[-1],name='forged')
+            else: kwargs=dict(kwargs,response_format=dict(type='object'))
+        return original(leaf,messages,**kwargs)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(server._NativeResearchLeaf,'generate',mutate)
+    if fault=='skip_guard':
+        borrow=server._borrow_research
+        def skip(*a,**kw):
+            view,client=borrow(*a,**kw); view.inner=view.leaf
+            return view,client
+        monkeypatch.setattr(server,'_borrow_research',skip)
+    elif fault=='secured_output':
+        from openjarvis.security.guardrails import GuardrailsEngine
+        generate=GuardrailsEngine.generate
+        turns=[0]
+        def redact(self,*a,**kw):
+            result=generate(self,*a,**kw); turns[0]+=1
+            if turns[0]==4: result=dict(result,content='{"final_answer":"changed secured envelope"}')
+            return result
+        monkeypatch.setattr(GuardrailsEngine,'generate',redact)
+    elif fault in ('raw_unknown','unsafe_unknown'):
+        def bad(body):
+            content=navigation_reply(body)
+            if fault=='unsafe_unknown': content=json.dumps(dict(json.loads(content),extra='password = syntheticsecret'))
+            return httpx.Response(200,stream=httpx.ByteStream(json.dumps(dict(model='qwen3-8b-uncensored',done=True,
+                done_reason='stop',prompt_eval_count=25,eval_count=10,message=dict(role='assistant',content=content),
+                **({'unexpected':True} if fault=='raw_unknown' else {}))).encode()))
+        bad.no_auto_navigation=True
+        replies[3]=bad
+    result=submit(tmp_path,sdk_io[0],runner)
+    ledger=RESTRICTED[result['task_id']]
+    assert result['status']=='failed' and not list(tmp_path.rglob('research.md'))
+    assert len(ledger._issued)==(0 if fault=='skip_guard' else 2)
+    assert len(bodies)==(0 if fault=='skip_guard' else 4 if fault in ('secured_output','raw_unknown','unsafe_unknown') else 3)
+    assert 'syntheticsecret' not in json.dumps(result)
+
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('status,headers',[(500,{}),(200,{'content-encoding':'gzip'}),(200,{'content-length':'32769'}),(200,{'content-length':'-1'})])
+def test_v3_header_rejection_does_not_consume_body(status,headers):
+    consumed=[]
+    class Stream(httpx.SyncByteStream):
+        def __iter__(self): consumed.append(True); yield b'x'*4096
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline=time.monotonic()+260
+    with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(
+            lambda _:httpx.Response(status,headers=headers,stream=Stream())),trust_env=False) as client:
+        transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError): transport.exchange('GET','/api/version','metadata')
+    receipt=transport.receipts[0]
+    assert not consumed and receipt['inspected']==receipt['admitted']==receipt['downloaded']==0

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind,cap',[('metadata',32768),('chat',65536)])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('delta',[-1,0,1])
+def test_v3_response_envelope_boundaries(kind,cap,delta):
+    class Stream(httpx.SyncByteStream):
+        def __iter__(self): yield b'x'*(cap+delta)
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline=time.monotonic()+260
+    with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(
+            lambda _:httpx.Response(200,stream=Stream())),trust_env=False) as client:
+        transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+        if delta>0:
+            with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='overflow'): transport.exchange('GET','/api/version',kind)
+        else: assert len(transport.exchange('GET','/api/version',kind))==cap+delta
+    receipt=transport.receipts[0]
+    assert receipt['admitted']==min(cap,cap+delta)
+    assert receipt['inspected']==cap+delta
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('boundary',['entity','total','posts','gets'])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('delta',[-1,0,1])
+def test_v3_envelopes_deadlines_and_effects(boundary,delta):
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline=time.monotonic()+260
+    sent=[]
+    def handle(request):
+        sent.append(request)
+        return httpx.Response(200,stream=httpx.ByteStream(b'{}'))
+    with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle),trust_env=False) as client:
+        transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+        method='POST'; kind='chat'; path='/api/chat'; entity=b'{}'
+        if boundary=='entity': entity=b'x'*(49152+delta)
+        elif boundary=='total': transport.post_bytes=393216-len(entity)+delta
+        elif boundary=='posts': transport.posts=7+delta
+        else: method='GET'; kind='metadata'; path='/api/version'; entity=b''; transport.gets=15+delta
+        if delta>0:
+            with pytest.raises(ValueError): transport.exchange(method,path,kind,entity=entity)
+            assert not sent
+        else: assert transport.exchange(method,path,kind,entity=entity)==b'{}' and len(sent)==1
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure',['connect','partial','partial_close','close'])
+def test_v3_failed_stream_reservation_not_refunded(failure):
+    ledger=wt.WorkLedger(); ledger.deadline=time.monotonic()+260
+    class Stream(httpx.SyncByteStream):
+        def __iter__(self):
+            yield b'x'*512
+            if failure.startswith('partial'): raise httpx.ReadError('partial response')
+        def close(self):
+            if 'close' in failure: raise RuntimeError('close error')
+    def handle(request):
+        if failure=='connect': raise httpx.ConnectError('connect error',request=request)
+        return httpx.Response(200,stream=Stream())
+    with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:11434',transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle),trust_env=False) as client:
+        transport=server._ResearchTransport(client,ledger)
+        with pytest.raises((httpx.ConnectError,httpx.ReadError,RuntimeError)):
+            transport.exchange('POST','/api/chat','chat',entity=b'{}')
+    receipt=transport.receipts[0]
+    assert transport.posts==1 and transport.post_bytes==2 and transport.completed==0
+    assert receipt['downloaded']==(None if failure=='connect' else 512)
+    assert receipt['admitted']==(0 if failure=='connect' else 512)
+    assert receipt['error']==('ConnectError' if failure=='connect' else 'ReadError' if failure.startswith('partial') else 'RuntimeError')
+

 def test_cross_request_catalogue_is_rejected(tmp_path,sdk_io):

+    assert len(captured)==len(bodies)==5
     for full,body in zip(captured,bodies):
         # Compare every path/value independently; only schema-keyword lengths disappear.
         removed=[]
         def compare(old,new,path=()):
             if isinstance(old,dict):
                 assert set(new)==set(old)-{'maxLength'},path
                 for key,value in old.items():
                     if key=='maxLength': removed.append((path,value))
                     else: compare(value,new[key],path+(key,))
             elif isinstance(old,list):
                 assert len(old)==len(new)
                 for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(old,new)): compare(a,b,path+(i,))
             else: assert old==new,path
         compare(full,body['format'])
         assert removed

+    assert captured[4]['properties']['final_answer']['maxLength']==1000

 @pytest.fixture
 def source9_clock(mock_jar, monkeypatch):
     """Controlled elapsed time around real HTTPX MockTransport and SDK calls."""
     import httpx
     from src.brain import agent_server, work_service, work_tools
-    state = dict(now=0, metadata=0, author=0, ack=0, overrun=False, requests=[])
+    state = dict(now=0, metadata=0, author=0, ack=0, overrun=False, writer_only=False, requests=[])
     monkeypatch.setattr(work_service, 'monotonic', lambda: 0)
     monkeypatch.setattr(work_tools, 'monotonic', lambda: state['now'])
+    from src.brain import work_navigation
+    from types import SimpleNamespace
+    monkeypatch.setattr(work_navigation,'time',SimpleNamespace(monotonic=lambda:state['now']))
     factory = agent_server._research_client
     def client():
         result = factory()
         def observe(request):
             timeout = dict(request.extensions['timeout'])
             phase = ('author' if 'anyOf' in json.loads(request.content)['format'] else 'ack') if request.method=='POST' else 'metadata'
             state['requests'].append((phase, timeout))
             elapsed = state[phase]
+            if phase=='author' and state['writer_only'] and not any(a.get('properties',{}).get('action',{}).get('enum')==['ultima_work_research_write'] for a in json.loads(request.content)['format'].get('anyOf',[])):
+                elapsed=0
             state['now'] += elapsed
             if elapsed > timeout['read'] and not state['overrun']:
                 raise httpx.ReadTimeout('scripted inactivity timeout', request=request)
         result.event_hooks['request'].append(observe)
         return result
     monkeypatch.setattr(agent_server, '_research_client', client)
     return state

+"""Safe immutable navigation data and restricted replay; no runtime authority."""
+from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, replace
+import hashlib
+from html.parser import HTMLParser
+import inspect
+import json
+from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
+import platform
+import re
+import secrets
+import time
+import tokenize
+from urllib.parse import urlsplit, urlunsplit
+import uuid
+
+BindingKey = tuple[str, str, str]
+EXTRACTOR_SHA256 = '9d7cab4749f27dbc08f1b246e03c8a6f8a059acb74692802a1326216e00ef86d'
+DOC_SUFFIXES = ('', '.md', '.rst', '.txt', '.htm', '.html', '.adoc')
+
+
+def canonical_v3(value: object) -> bytes:
+    def check(v):
+        if v is None or type(v) in (bool, int): return
+        if type(v) is str:
+            if any(0xd800 <= ord(c) <= 0xdfff for c in v): raise ValueError('Surrogate in canonical JSON')
+            return
+        if type(v) is list:
+            for item in v: check(item)
+            return
+        if type(v) is dict and all(type(k) is str for k in v):
+            for k, item in v.items(): check(k); check(item)
+            return
+        raise ValueError('Unsupported canonical JSON type')
+    check(value)
+    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf8')
+
+
+def _h(raw):
+    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
+
+
+def _load(raw):
+    obj = json.loads(raw)
+    if canonical_v3(obj) != raw: raise ValueError('Noncanonical sealed JSON')
+    return obj
+
+
+def _uuid(value):
+    if type(value) is not str or str(uuid.UUID(value)) != value: raise ValueError('Noncanonical UUID')
+    return value
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class ScanPolicy:
+    scanners: tuple[object, ...]
+    mode: str
+    policy_json: bytes
+    policy_sha256: str
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class SourceV3:
+    source_id: str
+    identity_json: bytes
+    domain: str
+    suffix: str
+    total_lines: int
+    safe_line_pairs: tuple[tuple[int, str], ...]
+    omissions: tuple[tuple[int, int, str], ...]
+    anchors_json: bytes
+    available: bool
+    reason: str
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CatalogueV3:
+    binding_key: BindingKey
+    nonce: str
+    policy_sha256: str
+    sources: tuple[SourceV3, ...]
+    catalogue_json: bytes
+    catalogue_sha256: str
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class PageKey:
+    generation: int
+    seq: int
+    dispatch_uuid: str
+    adapter_uuid: str
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class ViewV3:
+    view_id: str
+    source_id: str
+    start: int
+    end: int
+    wire_json: bytes
+    wire_sha256: str
+    cards_json: bytes
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class IssuedSnapshotV3:
+    binding_key: BindingKey
+    nonce: str
+    catalogue_sha256: str
+    generation: int
+    views_json: bytes
+    replay_json: bytes
+    manifest_sha256: str
+
+
+def _policy_data(scanners, mode):
+    if type(scanners) is not tuple or not scanners or mode not in ('block', 'warn', 'redact'):
+        raise ValueError('Missing configured scanner policy')
+    extractor_path=Path(__file__).with_name('browser')/'channel.py'
+    if _h(extractor_path.read_bytes())!=EXTRACTOR_SHA256:
+        raise ValueError('Pinned BrowserChannel extractor drift')
+    descriptors=[]
+    for scanner in scanners:
+        cls=type(scanner)
+        if not callable(getattr(scanner,'scan',None)) or not callable(getattr(scanner,'redact',None)):
+            raise ValueError('Invalid scanner')
+        path=inspect.getsourcefile(cls)
+        if not path: raise ValueError('Scanner source identity unavailable')
+        backend=getattr(scanner,'_rust_impl',None)
+        backend_module=inspect.getmodule(type(backend)) if backend is not None else None
+        backend_path=getattr(backend_module,'__file__',None)
+        descriptors.append(dict(name=cls.__module__+'.'+cls.__qualname__,source_sha256=_h(Path(path).read_bytes()),
+            backend=type(backend).__module__+'.'+type(backend).__qualname__,
+            backend_sha256=_h(Path(backend_path).read_bytes()) if backend_path else None,
+            patterns=repr(getattr(scanner,'PATTERNS',None))))
+    return dict(v=3,scanners=descriptors,mode=mode,extractor=EXTRACTOR_SHA256,
+        navigation_sha256=_h(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
+        witness_sha256=_h(Path(__file__).with_name('work_research.py').read_bytes()),
+        python=platform.python_version(),htmlparser=_h(Path(inspect.getsourcefile(HTMLParser)).read_bytes()),
+        witness='source9-unchanged',serialization='J3',paging=[262144,1024,4096,3072,24,8,8],render='issued-page-v3')
+
+
+def make_scan_policy(scanners: tuple[object, ...], mode: str) -> ScanPolicy:
+    raw=canonical_v3(_policy_data(scanners,mode))
+    return ScanPolicy(scanners,mode,raw,_h(raw))
+
+
+def validate_scan_policy(policy: ScanPolicy, expected: ScanPolicy | None = None):
+    raw=canonical_v3(_policy_data(policy.scanners,policy.mode))
+    if raw!=policy.policy_json or _h(raw)!=policy.policy_sha256: raise ValueError('Scanner policy drift')
+    if expected is not None and (policy.policy_json!=expected.policy_json or len(policy.scanners)!=len(expected.scanners)
+            or any(a is not b for a,b in zip(policy.scanners,expected.scanners))):
+        raise ValueError('Scanner object/order drift')
+
+
+def _findings(text, policy):
+    return any(not s.scan(text).clean for s in policy.scanners)
+
+
+def _redact(text, policy):
+    for scanner in policy.scanners: text=scanner.redact(text)
+    return text
+
+
+def scan_view(view: ViewV3, policy: ScanPolicy) -> ViewV3:
+    """Mandatory final observation check by the trusted inspector before return."""
+    validate_scan_policy(policy)
+    def unsafe(value):
+        if isinstance(value,str): return _findings(value,policy)
+        if isinstance(value,list): return any(unsafe(v) for v in value)
+        if isinstance(value,dict): return any(unsafe(k) or unsafe(v) for k,v in value.items())
+        return False
+    if (_h(view.wire_json)!=view.wire_sha256 or _findings(view.wire_json.decode('utf8'),policy)
+            or unsafe(_load(view.wire_json))):
+        raise ValueError('Unsafe complete observation')
+    return view
+
+
+def _projection(body, kind):
+    if 'html' not in kind: return body
+    class Text(HTMLParser):
+        def __init__(self): super().__init__(); self.parts=[]; self.skip=0
+        def handle_starttag(self,tag,attrs):
+            if tag in ('script','style'): self.skip+=1
+        def handle_endtag(self,tag):
+            if tag in ('script','style'): self.skip=max(0,self.skip-1)
+        def handle_data(self,data):
+            if not self.skip: self.parts.append(data)
+    parser=Text(); parser.feed(body)
+    return ' '.join(parser.parts)
+
+
+def _normalize(url):
+    p=urlsplit(url)
+    if p.scheme not in ('http','https') or not p.hostname or p.username or p.password or p.fragment:
+        raise ValueError('Unsafe source identity')
+    host=p.hostname.lower()
+    if ':' in host: host='['+host+']'
+    if p.port not in (None,80 if p.scheme=='http' else 443): host+=':'+str(p.port)
+    return urlunsplit((p.scheme.lower(),host,p.path or '/',p.query,''))
+
+
+def _runs(omitted):
+    runs=[]
+    for line,reason in sorted(omitted.items()):
+        if runs and runs[-1][1]+1==line and runs[-1][2]==reason: runs[-1][1]=line
+        else: runs.append([line,line,reason])
+    return runs
+
+
+def _linked_sources(collection, binding, records):
+    from .work_research import bound_sources
+    sources=list(bound_sources(collection.catalogue_json,binding).values())
+    if len(sources)>5: raise ValueError('Too many sources')
+    attempts=json.loads(collection.attempts_json); trace=json.loads(collection.trace_json)
+    for index,source in enumerate(sources,1):
+        if source['source_id']!='S'+str(index): raise ValueError('Invalid source labels')
+        call=_uuid(source['read_call_id'])
+        matching=[r for r in records if r.get('call_id')==call]
+        if len(matching)!=1: raise ValueError('GET ledger mismatch')
+        record=matching[0]
+        ids={k:getattr(binding,k) for k in ('request_id','session_id','task_id')}
+        if record.get('tool_name')!='ultima_work_http_get' or record.get('success') is not True or any(record.get(k)!=v for k,v in ids.items()):
+            raise ValueError('GET binding mismatch')
+        original={k:v for k,v in source.items() if k not in (*ids,'read_call_id','adapter_source_id')}
+        original['source_id']=source['adapter_source_id']
+        if record.get('result')!=original: raise ValueError('GET original result mismatch')
+        matches=[a for a in attempts if a.get('adapter_call_id')==call and a.get('tool_name')=='ultima_work_http_get']
+        if len(matches)!=1 or matches[0].get('success') is not True or any(matches[0].get(k)!=v for k,v in ids.items()):
+            raise ValueError('GET attempt linkage mismatch')
+        sdk=_uuid(matches[0]['sdk_call_id'])
+        events=[e for e in trace if e.get('sdk_call_id')==sdk]
+        if [e.get('type') for e in events]!=['tool_call_start','tool_call_end'] or any(any(e.get(k)!=v for k,v in ids.items()) or e.get('data',{}).get('tool')!='ultima_work_http_get' for e in events):
+            raise ValueError('GET event linkage mismatch')
+        start,end=events[0]['data'],events[1]['data']
+        metadata=end.get('metadata',{})
+        if (start.get('arguments')!={'url':source['requested_url']} or end.get('success') is not True
+                or metadata.get('call_id')!=call or metadata.get('result')!=record['result']
+                or metadata.get('success') is not True or any(metadata.get(k)!=v for k,v in ids.items())):
+            raise ValueError('GET START/END result linkage mismatch')
+        if _h(source['body'].encode('utf8'))!=source['decoded_body_sha256']: raise ValueError('Body hash drift')
+        if _normalize(source['final_url'])!=source['dedupe_url']: raise ValueError('Normalized URL mismatch')
+        if _projection(source['body'],source.get('content_type',''))!=source['text']: raise ValueError('Projection hash drift')
+    if len({s['dedupe_url'] for s in sources})!=len(sources) or len({s['decoded_body_sha256'] for s in sources})!=len(sources):
+        raise ValueError('Duplicate source URL/body')
+    return sources
+
+
+def _safe_source(source, policy):
+    from .work_research import _anchors, _python_witnesses, _js_tokens, _fact_evidence
+    body,text=source['body'],source['text']
+    domain='extracted-text' if 'html' in source.get('content_type','') else 'raw'
+    suffix=PurePosixPath(urlsplit(source['final_url']).path).suffix.lower()
+    identity=[source['source_id'],source['final_url'],source['dedupe_url'],source['read_call_id'],
+              source['decoded_body_sha256'],_h(text.encode('utf8')),domain,policy.policy_sha256]
+    if any(_findings(value,policy) for value in identity) or _findings(canonical_v3(identity).decode(),policy):
+        raise ValueError('Unsafe source provenance identity')
+    rows=text.splitlines(); reason=''; safe=[]; omitted={}; anchors=[]
+    if source.get('truncated'): reason='get-truncated'
+    elif len(body.encode('utf8'))>262144 or len(text.encode('utf8'))>262144: reason='source-size'
+    elif len(body.splitlines())>1024 or len(rows)>1024: reason='source-lines'
+    elif any(re.search(r'\r(?!\n)|[\v\f\x1c-\x1e\x85\u2028\u2029]',v) for v in (body,text)): reason='unsupported-coordinates'
+    elif domain=='extracted-text' and _findings(body,policy): reason='unsafe-html'
+    elif domain=='extracted-text' and suffix not in DOC_SUFFIXES: reason='unsupported-suffix'
+    elif policy.mode=='block' and _findings(text,policy): reason='security-block'
+    else:
+        segments=text.splitlines(keepends=True)
+        redacted=_redact(text,policy)
+        if redacted!=''.join(_redact(line,policy) for line in segments) or _findings(redacted,policy):
+            reason='security-map'
+        else:
+            for i,line in enumerate(rows,1):
+                if _findings(line,policy) or _redact(line,policy)!=line: omitted[i]='security'
+                else: safe.append((i,line))
+            visible=dict(safe); syntax=None
+            try:
+                if suffix=='.py': syntax=list(_python_witnesses(body))
+                elif suffix in ('.js','.ts','.mjs','.cjs'): syntax=_js_tokens(body,suffix)
+                for a,z,kind,op in sorted(set(_anchors(source,visible,syntax))):
+                    if not 1<=a<=z<a+8 or any(i not in visible for i in range(a,z+1)): continue
+                    witness=dict(claim=op or 'witness',kind=kind,first_line=a,last_line=z)
+                    if kind=='code': witness['symbol']=op
+                    _fact_evidence(witness,source,visible,syntax)
+                    if not _findings(op,policy): anchors.append([a,z,kind,op])
+            except (SyntaxError,ValueError,tokenize.TokenError):
+                anchors=[]
+    return SourceV3(source['source_id'],canonical_v3(identity),domain,suffix,len(rows),tuple(safe),
+                    tuple(tuple(r) for r in _runs(omitted)),canonical_v3(anchors),not bool(reason),reason)
+
+
+def build_page(catalogue: CatalogueV3, source_id: str, start_line: int, key: PageKey) -> ViewV3:
+    if not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}',catalogue.nonce): raise ValueError('Invalid nonce')
+    if len(catalogue.binding_key)!=3 or any(type(x) is not str or not x for x in catalogue.binding_key): raise ValueError('Invalid binding')
+    for n in (key.generation,key.seq):
+        if type(n) is not int or not 1<=n<=2147483647: raise ValueError('Invalid page sequence')
+    _uuid(key.dispatch_uuid); _uuid(key.adapter_uuid)
+    source=next((s for s in catalogue.sources if s.source_id==source_id),None)
+    if source is None or not source.available: raise ValueError('Unavailable source')
+    if type(start_line) is not int or not 1<=start_line<=source.total_lines: raise ValueError('Invalid start line')
+    end=min(source.total_lines,start_line+23)
+    view_id=_h(canonical_v3(['view3',catalogue.nonce,list(catalogue.binding_key),json.loads(source.identity_json),
+                           start_line,end,key.seq,key.dispatch_uuid,key.adapter_uuid]))
+    safe=dict(source.safe_line_pairs)
+    omitted={i:reason for a,z,reason in source.omissions for i in range(max(a,start_line),min(z,end)+1)}
+    wire=dict(v=3,source_id=source_id,view_id=view_id,start=start_line,end=end,total_lines=source.total_lines,
+              lines=[],cards=[],omissions=[],omitted_cards=0,next_start=end+1 if end<source.total_lines else None)
+    def encoded():
+        wire['omissions']=_runs(omitted)
+        return canonical_v3(wire)
+    for line in range(start_line,end+1):
+        if line not in safe: omitted.setdefault(line,'security')
+        else: omitted[line]='size'
+    if len(encoded())>3072: raise ValueError('Page metadata exceeds cap')
+    for line in range(start_line,end+1):
+        if line not in safe: continue
+        wire['lines'].append([line,safe[line]]); omitted.pop(line,None)
+        if len(encoded())>3072:
+            wire['lines'].pop(); omitted[line]='size'
+    visible=dict(wire['lines']); candidates=[]
+    for a,z,kind,op in json.loads(source.anchors_json):
+        if not start_line<=a<=z<=end or any(i not in visible for i in range(a,z+1)): continue
+        left,right=a,z; padding=7-(z-a)
+        for _ in range(padding//2):
+            if left-1 not in visible: break
+            left-=1
+        for _ in range(padding-padding//2):
+            if right+1 not in visible: break
+            right+=1
+        ap=[[i,visible[i]] for i in range(a,z+1)]; cp=[[i,visible[i]] for i in range(left,right+1)]
+        ref='N3.'+_h(canonical_v3(['card3',view_id,kind,op,a,z,left,right,_h(canonical_v3(ap)),_h(canonical_v3(cp))]))[:16]
+        card=[ref,kind,op,a,z,left,right]
+        if any(old[0]==ref for old in candidates): raise ValueError('Reference collision')
+        candidates.append(card)
+    wire['omitted_cards']=len(candidates)
+    # Metadata digit growth can require dropping trailing lines; never cut a line.
+    while len(encoded())>3072 and wire['lines']:
+        line,_=wire['lines'].pop(); omitted[line]='size'
+    visible=dict(wire['lines'])
+    candidates=[c for c in candidates if all(i in visible for i in range(c[5],c[6]+1))]
+    total=len(candidates)
+    wire['omitted_cards']=total
+    for card in candidates:
+        if len(wire['cards'])==8: break
+        wire['cards'].append(card); wire['omitted_cards']=total-len(wire['cards'])
+        if len(encoded())>3072:
+            wire['cards'].pop(); wire['omitted_cards']=total-len(wire['cards']); break
+    raw=encoded()
+    if len(raw)>3072: raise ValueError('Page fit failure')
+    return ViewV3(view_id,source_id,start_line,end,raw,_h(raw),canonical_v3(wire['cards']))
+
+
+def prepare_navigation(collection, binding, scanners: ScanPolicy, *, get_records_json: bytes, deadline: float) -> CatalogueV3:
+    if time.monotonic()>=deadline: raise ValueError('Navigation preparation deadline')
+    validate_scan_policy(scanners)
+    records=json.loads(get_records_json)
+    originals=_linked_sources(collection,binding,records)
+    sources=tuple(_safe_source(s,scanners) for s in originals)
+    b=tuple(getattr(binding,k) for k in ('request_id','session_id','task_id'))
+    nonce=secrets.token_hex(16)
+    cat=CatalogueV3(b,nonce,scanners.policy_sha256,sources,b'', '')
+    available=[]
+    for source in sources:
+        fits=False
+        if source.available:
+            for start in range(1,source.total_lines+1):
+                if time.monotonic()>=deadline: raise ValueError('Navigation preparation deadline')
+                view=build_page(cat,source.source_id,start,PageKey(1,2147483647,str(uuid.UUID(int=1)),str(uuid.UUID(int=2))))
+                # Placeholder IDs prove byte fit only. Actual final wire is
+                # scanned by the trusted read port before staging or exposure.
+                if json.loads(view.cards_json): fits=True; break
+        available.append(replace(source,available=fits,reason=source.reason or ('' if fits else 'unrenderable')))
+    sources=tuple(available); cat=replace(cat,sources=sources)
+    if sum(s.available for s in sources)<3: raise ValueError('Research needs three renderable distinct sources before composition')
+    entries=[]
+    for source in sources:
+        anchors=json.loads(source.anchors_json)
+        eligible=anchors if len(anchors)<=8 else [anchors[round(i*(len(anchors)-1)/7)] for i in range(8)]
+        landmarks=[[a,z,op] if source.suffix not in DOC_SUFFIXES else [a,z] for a,z,_,op in eligible] if source.available else []
+        url=json.loads(source.identity_json)[1]; p=urlsplit(url)
+        label=p.netloc+p.path
+        if _findings(label,scanners): raise ValueError('Unsafe catalogue identity')
+        entries.append(dict(source_id=source.source_id,identity=label,suffix=source.suffix,total_lines=source.total_lines,
+                            available=source.available,reason=source.reason,landmarks=landmarks))
+    while len(canonical_v3(entries))>4096:
+        changed=False
+        for entry in reversed(entries):
+            if entry['landmarks']:
+                entry['landmarks'].pop(); changed=True
+                if len(canonical_v3(entries))<=4096: break
+        if not changed: raise ValueError('Mandatory catalogue identities exceed cap')
+    raw=canonical_v3(entries)
+    if _findings(raw.decode('utf8'),scanners): raise ValueError('Unsafe catalogue')
+    validate_scan_policy(scanners)
+    return replace(cat,catalogue_json=raw,catalogue_sha256=_h(raw))
+
+
+def v3_writer_schema() -> dict:
+    def obj(**props): return dict(type='object',additionalProperties=False,properties=props,required=list(props))
+    def text(n): return dict(type='string',minLength=1,maxLength=n)
+    def array(items,a,z): return dict(type='array',items=items,minItems=a,maxItems=z)
+    fact=obj(claim=text(2000),evidence_id=dict(type='string',pattern=r'^N3\.[0-9a-f]{16}$',minLength=19,maxLength=19))
+    section=obj(source_id=dict(type='string',pattern=r'^S[1-5]$',minLength=2,maxLength=2),heading=text(200),facts=array(fact,1,6))
+    return obj(path=text(2048),title=text(200),sections=array(section,3,5))
+
+
+def _source_packet(source):
+    result=asdict(source)
+    for k in ('identity_json','anchors_json'): result[k]=json.loads(result[k])
+    result['safe_line_pairs']=[list(x) for x in source.safe_line_pairs]
+    result['omissions']=[list(x) for x in source.omissions]
+    return result
+

+def seal_snapshot(catalogue, collection, get_records_json, policy, reads, *, generation):
+    """Seal already issued reads; caller ledger alone proves issuance authority."""
+    validate_scan_policy(policy)
+    if not 1<=len(reads)<=6: raise ValueError('Invalid read count')
+    records=[]; seen=set()
+    for key,view in reads:
+        if (view.source_id,view.start) in seen or key.generation>=generation: raise ValueError('Unissued or duplicate page')
+        seen.add((view.source_id,view.start))
+        expected=build_page(catalogue,view.source_id,view.start,key)
+        if expected!=view: raise ValueError('Read wire mismatch')
+        scan_view(view,policy)
+        records.append(dict(key=asdict(key),source_id=view.source_id,start=view.start,wire=json.loads(view.wire_json),wire_sha256=view.wire_sha256))
+    packet=dict(v=3,binding_key=list(catalogue.binding_key),nonce=catalogue.nonce,policy=json.loads(policy.policy_json),
+        catalogue=json.loads(catalogue.catalogue_json),catalogue_sha256=catalogue.catalogue_sha256,
+        sources=[_source_packet(s) for s in catalogue.sources],collection=asdict(collection),get_records=json.loads(get_records_json),reads=records,generation=generation)
+    packet['collection']['ignored_failure_ids']=list(collection.ignored_failure_ids)
+    raw=canonical_v3(packet)
+    views=canonical_v3([r['wire'] for r in records])
+    return IssuedSnapshotV3(catalogue.binding_key,catalogue.nonce,catalogue.catalogue_sha256,generation,views,raw,_h(raw))
+

+def resolve_v3(submitted: dict, issued_snapshot: IssuedSnapshotV3, collection) -> tuple[dict, list[dict]]:
+    from jsonschema import Draft7Validator
+    from types import SimpleNamespace
+    from .work_research import _fact_evidence
+    snapshot=issued_snapshot
+    if _h(snapshot.replay_json)!=snapshot.manifest_sha256: raise ValueError('Replay manifest drift')
+    packet=_load(snapshot.replay_json)
+    if packet.get('v')!=3 or packet['binding_key']!=list(snapshot.binding_key) or packet['nonce']!=snapshot.nonce or packet['generation']!=snapshot.generation or packet['catalogue_sha256']!=snapshot.catalogue_sha256:
+        raise ValueError('Foreign snapshot binding')
+    original=asdict(collection); original['ignored_failure_ids']=list(collection.ignored_failure_ids)
+    if packet['collection']!=original: raise ValueError('Original collection drift')
+    # Restore the real configured SDK scanners from the sealed policy; no substitute scanner.
+    from openjarvis.security.scanner import SecretScanner, PIIScanner
+    factories={c.__module__+'.'+c.__qualname__:c for c in (SecretScanner,PIIScanner)}
+    try: scanners=tuple(factories[d['name']]() for d in packet['policy']['scanners'])
+    except KeyError as exc: raise ValueError('Unknown replay scanner backend') from exc
+    policy=make_scan_policy(scanners,packet['policy']['mode'])
+    if json.loads(policy.policy_json)!=packet['policy']: raise ValueError('Replay policy drift')
+    binding=SimpleNamespace(**dict(zip(('request_id','session_id','task_id'),snapshot.binding_key)))
+    cat=prepare_navigation(collection,binding,policy,get_records_json=canonical_v3(packet['get_records']),deadline=time.monotonic()+30)
+    cat=replace(cat,nonce=snapshot.nonce)
+    if cat.catalogue_sha256!=snapshot.catalogue_sha256 or json.loads(cat.catalogue_json)!=packet['catalogue'] or [_source_packet(s) for s in cat.sources]!=packet['sources']:
+        raise ValueError('Safe source reconstruction drift')
+    reads=packet['reads']
+    if not 1<=len(reads)<=6 or canonical_v3([r['wire'] for r in reads])!=snapshot.views_json: raise ValueError('Issued views drift')
+    issued={}; seen=set(); sequences=set(); dispatches=set(); adapters=set()
+    for read in reads:
+        key=PageKey(**read['key']); source_id=read['source_id']; start=read['start']
+        if key.generation>=snapshot.generation or key.seq in sequences or key.dispatch_uuid in dispatches or key.adapter_uuid in adapters or (source_id,start) in seen:
+            raise ValueError('Duplicate or unissued read')
+        sequences.add(key.seq); dispatches.add(key.dispatch_uuid); adapters.add(key.adapter_uuid); seen.add((source_id,start))
+        view=scan_view(build_page(cat,source_id,start,key),policy)
+        if json.loads(view.wire_json)!=read['wire'] or view.wire_sha256!=read['wire_sha256']: raise ValueError('Issued page replay drift')
+        for card in json.loads(view.cards_json):
+            ref=card[0]
+            if ref in issued: raise ValueError('Reference collision')
+            issued[ref]=(card,view)
+    if not Draft7Validator(v3_writer_schema()).is_valid(submitted):
+        raise ValueError('Invalid v3 submitted schema')
+    resolved=json.loads(canonical_v3(submitted)); bindings=[]; sources={s['source_id']:s for s in json.loads(collection.catalogue_json)}
+    used=[]
+    for section in resolved['sections']:
+        sid=section['source_id']
+        if sid in used or sid not in sources: raise ValueError('Duplicate or unknown source section')
+        used.append(sid)
+        for fact in section['facts']:
+            ref=fact.pop('evidence_id')
+            if ref not in issued: raise ValueError('Unknown or unissued reference')
+            card,view=issued[ref]
+            if view.source_id!=sid: raise ValueError('Foreign reference')
+            _,kind,op,a,z,left,right=card
+            fact.update(kind=kind,first_line=a,last_line=z)
+            if kind=='code': fact['symbol']=op
+            visible=dict(json.loads(view.wire_json)['lines'])
+            _fact_evidence(fact,sources[sid],visible)
+            source=sources[sid]
+            binding_card={k:source[k] for k in ('source_id','final_url','decoded_body_sha256','read_call_id','request_id','session_id','task_id')}
+            binding_card.update(evidence_id=ref,kind=kind,symbol=op,first_line=a,last_line=z,citation_first=left,citation_last=right,
+                domain=next(s.domain for s in cat.sources if s.source_id==sid),view_id=view.view_id,view_json=view.wire_json.decode())
+            bindings.append(binding_card)
+    if len({sources[s]['dedupe_url'] for s in used})<3 or len({sources[s]['decoded_body_sha256'] for s in used})<3: raise ValueError('Three distinct sources required')
+    return resolved,bindings
+

+def render_v3(payload, sources_json, *, bindings):
+    from .work_research import RenderedReport, bound_sources, _text, _escape, _fact_evidence
+    sources=bound_sources(sources_json); lines=['# '+_escape(_text(payload['title'],200)),'']; evidence=[]; cited=[]
+    index=0
+    for section in payload['sections']:
+        sid=section['source_id']; source=sources[sid]
+        if sid in cited: raise ValueError('Duplicate section')
+        lines+=['## '+_escape(_text(section['heading'],200)),source['final_url'],'']
+        for fact in section['facts']:
+            card=bindings[index]; index+=1
+            if card['source_id']!=sid: raise ValueError('Foreign citation')
+            view=json.loads(card['view_json']); visible=dict(view['lines'])
+            support=_fact_evidence(fact,source,visible)
+            left,right=card['citation_first'],card['citation_last']
+            support.update(citation_first=left,citation_last=right,citation_text='\n'.join(visible[i] for i in range(left,right+1)),domain=card['domain'])
+            evidence.append(support)
+            label={'documented':'文档说明','code':'代码观察','limitation':'该处标注'}[support['kind']]
+            lines.append(f"- {label}（{card['domain']} L{left}–L{right}，依据 L{support['first_line']}–L{support['last_line']}）"+_escape(support['claim']))
+        cited.append(sid); lines.append('')
+    if index!=len(bindings) or len(cited)<3 or len({sources[s]['dedupe_url'] for s in cited})<3 or len({sources[s]['decoded_body_sha256'] for s in cited})<3:
+        raise ValueError('Invalid v3 rendering bindings')
+    return RenderedReport('\n'.join(lines),tuple(cited),tuple(evidence))

+def checked_native_identity(version_bytes,tags_bytes,manifest_digest):
+    from .agent_server import _strict_json,_RESEARCH_MODEL,_RESEARCH_DIGEST
+    if not all(type(b) is bytes and 0<len(b)<=32768 for b in (version_bytes,tags_bytes)):
+        raise ValueError('Invalid metadata identity bytes')
+    version=_strict_json(version_bytes.decode()); tags=_strict_json(tags_bytes.decode())
+    if version!={'version':'0.32.15'} or set(tags)!={'models'} or not isinstance(tags['models'],list):
+        raise ValueError('Unsupported native metadata identity')
+    matches=[]
+    for model in tags['models']:
+        if (not isinstance(model,dict) or set(model)-{'name','model','modified_at','size','digest','details'}
+                or not all(type(model.get(k)) is str for k in ('name','digest'))
+                or ('size' in model and (type(model['size']) is not int or not 0<=model['size']<2**63))
+                or ('model' in model and model['model']!=model['name'])):
+            raise ValueError('Invalid native model metadata')
+        if model['name'] in (_RESEARCH_MODEL,_RESEARCH_MODEL+':latest'): matches.append(model['digest'])
+    if matches!=[_RESEARCH_DIGEST] or manifest_digest!=_RESEARCH_DIGEST:
+        raise ValueError('Unexpected research manifest identity')
+    return version,tags

+def checked_native_response(raw,max_tokens):

+            or any(type(n) is not int or n<1 for n in counts) or counts[1]>max_tokens or sum(counts)>16384
+            or any(type(raw[k]) is not int or not 0<=raw[k]<2**63 for k in ('total_duration','load_duration','prompt_eval_duration','eval_duration') if k in raw)

+def _verify_runtime_order(packet,reads,written,require_ack,runtime_inputs):
+    from math import isfinite
+    import uuid
+    from .work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    if packet['clock_domain']!='work_tools.monotonic;ledger-sequence-v1': raise ValueError('Runtime clock domain drift')
+    timeline=packet['timeline']; cursor=0; previous=None
+    def take(kind,**fields):
+        nonlocal cursor,previous
+        if cursor>=len(timeline): raise ValueError('Missing runtime admission order')
+        node=timeline[cursor]; cursor+=1
+        if (set(node)!={'order','kind','at','deadline',*fields} or node['order']!=cursor or node['kind']!=kind
+                or any(node[k]!=v for k,v in fields.items()) or node['deadline']!=runtime_inputs.deadline):
+            raise ValueError('Runtime admission order drift')
+        try:
+            if type(node['at']) is not str or type(node['deadline']) is not str: raise ValueError('Clock representation')
+            now=float(node['at']);deadline=float(node['deadline']);remaining=deadline-now
+        except (ValueError,TypeError,OverflowError): raise ValueError('Invalid runtime clock') from None
+        if not all(isfinite(n) for n in (now,deadline,remaining)) or (previous is not None and now<previous):
+            raise ValueError('Runtime monotonic clock drift')
+        previous=now
+        if remaining<=0: raise ValueError('Runtime deadline exceeded')
+        return node,remaining
+    for index,wire in enumerate(packet['wires']):
+        ack=index==len(reads)+1
+        take('generation',generation=index+1)
+        for receipt_index in range(index*3,index*3+3):
+            _,remaining=take('send',generation=index+1,receipt_index=receipt_index)
+            if not ack and remaining<=55: raise ValueError('Runtime send reserve exhausted')
+            receipt=packet['transport']['receipts'][receipt_index]
+            budget=remaining if ack else remaining-55
+            # Recorded repr strings round-trip the live binary64 samples. Keep
+            # its subtraction order so boundary timeouts have identical caps.
+            base=min(25,budget); read=min(25 if ack else 180,budget)
+            if receipt['kind']=='metadata': read=min(25,read)
+            timeout=json.loads(receipt['timeout_json'])
+            def admitted(value,limit):
+                try: return type(value) in (int,float) and isfinite(value) and 0<value<=limit
+                except OverflowError: return False
+            if (receipt['send_reserved'] is not True or receipt['admission_error'] is not None
+                    or set(timeout)!={'connect','read','write','pool'}
+                    or any(not admitted(timeout[k],limit) for k,limit in
+                        (('connect',base),('read',read),('write',base),('pool',base)))):
+                raise ValueError('Runtime final timeout admission drift')
+            take('return',generation=index+1,receipt_index=receipt_index)
+        _,remaining=take('secured',generation=index+1)
+        if not ack and remaining<55: raise ValueError('Runtime secured reserve exhausted')
+        if index and index<=len(reads): take('promote',seq=index,wire_hash=reads[index-1]['wire_sha256'])
+        if ack: continue
+        action=wire['secured_envelope']; seq=index+1
+        take('expect',seq=seq,name=action['action'],args_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical_v3(action['action_input'])).hexdigest())
+        take('sdk_start',seq=seq,event_index=index*2)
+        if index<len(reads): key=reads[index]['key']
+        elif written: key=packet['execution']['intent']
+        else:
+            if cursor>=len(timeline): raise ValueError('Missing writer claim')
+            key=timeline[cursor]
+        for field in ('dispatch_uuid','adapter_uuid'):
+            try:
+                if str(uuid.UUID(key[field]))!=key[field]: raise ValueError('Invalid claim UUID')
+            except (TypeError,KeyError): raise ValueError('Invalid claim UUID') from None
+        take('claim',seq=seq,dispatch_uuid=key['dispatch_uuid'],adapter_uuid=key['adapter_uuid'])
+        if index<len(reads): take('sdk_end',seq=seq,event_index=index*2+1)
+        else:
+            take('freeze',seq=seq)
+            if written:
+                _,remaining=take('intent',seq=seq,file_call_id=key['file_call_id'])
+                if remaining<55: raise ValueError('Runtime intent reserve exhausted')
+                take('file_end',file_call_id=key['file_call_id'])
+                take('sdk_end',seq=seq,event_index=index*2+1)
+    if cursor!=len(timeline): raise ValueError('Unexpected runtime terminal order')
+

+def verify_runtime_packet(binding, snapshot, collection, raw, *, require_ack=False,runtime_inputs=None):
+    """Reconstruct native entities, history and read/event links independently."""
+    from .work_navigation import canonical_v3,v3_writer_schema
+    from .work_tools import WorkInspectTool,WorkResearchWriteTool
+    from openjarvis.tools._stubs import ToolSpec,build_tool_descriptions

+    from .agent_server import _strict_json,_research_sampler_schema,_RESEARCH_MODEL,MAINBRAIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT

+    from openjarvis.security.scanner import SecretScanner,PIIScanner
+    from .work_navigation import make_scan_policy

+                or any(r['error'] is not None or not 200<=r['status']<300 or r['admitted']>cap

+                    or (r['downloaded'] is not None and r['downloaded']<r['admitted']) for r in group)):
+            raise ValueError('Runtime response accounting drift')
+    for index,wire in enumerate(wires):

+def verify_research_artifacts(binding,artifacts,report_records,sources_json,*,require_reference=False,
+                              required_version=None,issued_snapshot=None,runtime_packet=None,require_ack=False,runtime_inputs=None):
+    """Historical v2 readback or strict fresh v3 runtime/file verification."""
+    from .work_navigation import canonical_v3
+    fresh=(required_version==3 or require_ack or runtime_packet is not None
+        or any(r.get('result',{}).get('protocol_version')==3 for r in report_records))
+    if fresh:
+        if issued_snapshot is None or any(type(r.get('result',{}).get('protocol_version')) is not int
+                or r['result']['protocol_version']!=3 for r in report_records):
+            raise ValueError('Fresh protocol version 3 and snapshot required')
+        if type(runtime_packet) is not bytes or not runtime_packet:
+            raise ValueError('Nonempty immutable runtime bytes required')
+        runtime=verify_runtime_packet(binding,issued_snapshot,None,runtime_packet,require_ack=require_ack,runtime_inputs=runtime_inputs)
+        if runtime['execution']['intent'] is None: raise ValueError('Fresh runtime lacks file intent')
+        reports={r['result']['file_call_id']:r for r in report_records}
+        receipt=json.loads(json.loads(runtime['events'][-1])['data']['result'])
+        submitted=runtime['wires'][-2 if require_ack else -1]['secured_envelope']['action_input']
+        for artifact in artifacts:
+            record=reports.get(artifact['call_id'])
+            if record is None: raise ValueError('Missing runtime report adapter')
+            result=record['result']
+            if canonical_v3(json.loads(result['submitted_json']))!=canonical_v3(submitted):
+                raise ValueError('Runtime authored submission drift')
+            if (result.get('runtime_sha256')!=runtime['prewrite_sha256']
+                    or result.get('replay_sha256')!=issued_snapshot.manifest_sha256 or result.get('sha256')!=artifact['sha256']):
+                raise ValueError('Report runtime manifest linkage drift')
+            if (receipt['report_call_id']!=record['call_id'] or receipt['file_call_id']!=result['file_call_id']
+                    or receipt['dispatch_uuid']!=result['dispatch_uuid'] or receipt['adapter_uuid']!=result['adapter_uuid']
+                    or receipt['path']!=artifact['path'] or result['path']!=receipt['path'] or receipt['sha256']!=artifact['sha256']):
+                raise ValueError('Report runtime writer receipt drift')
+            if any((record['call_id'] if key=='report_call_id' else result.get(key))!=value for key,value in receipt.items()):
+                raise ValueError('Report original visible receipt field drift')
+        required_version=3
+    return _verify_artifact_data(binding,artifacts,report_records,sources_json,require_reference=require_reference,
+        required_version=required_version,issued_snapshot=issued_snapshot)

 @dataclass(frozen=True)
 class WorkCapabilities:
     provider: object = None
     capture_json: str | None = None
     replace: bool = False

+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class ResearchRuntimeInputs:
+    prompt: str
+    binding_key: tuple[str,str,str]
